" data-portal-copyright= " " **28;5057;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1243102 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Victoria&amp;rsquo ; s David Foster is letting online audiences into his home daily during the COVID-19 outbreak &amp;mdash ; albeit safely , via Instagram. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Grammy Award winner and his wife , Katharine McPhee , took to the social-media app this week for a concert from their living room , during which Foster addressed requests from the audience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We want to take requests for songs , but the thing is , I don&amp;rsquo ; t know how to play any songs ( by other people ) . We&amp;rsquo ; re new at this , &amp;rdquo ; Foster said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To watch the video , viewers first have to follow either Foster ( &lt;a href= " https : **32;5087;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;5121;TOOLONG ) or McPhee ( &lt;a href= " https : **33;5149;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;5184;TOOLONG ) on their personal Instagram pages . Foster said they&amp;rsquo ; ll try to livestream a concert at 5:30 p.m. each day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We decided because we&amp;rsquo ; re all housebound as most of you are &amp;mdash ; or should be &amp;mdash ; that we would have a little fun , &amp;rdquo ; Foster said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because we&amp;rsquo ; re bored , &amp;rdquo ; McPhee added . &amp;ldquo ; But we&amp;rsquo ; re sticking with the program for the greater good . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The couple performed Leonard Cohen&amp;rsquo ; s Hallelujah , Nat King Cole&amp;rsquo ; s We have no clue what we 're doing-gonna try and do some better camera angles -let us know what songs u want to hear-c u tomorrow at 5:30pm west coast time ! ! &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p style= " color : #c9c8cd ; font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; line-height:17px ; margin-bottom:0 ; margin-top:8px ; overflow:hidden ; padding:8px 0 7px ; text-align:center ; text-overflow:ellipsis ; white-space:nowrap ; " &gt;A post shared by &lt;a href= " https : **32;5467;TOOLONG ? ... style= " color : #c9c8cd ; font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; font-style:normal ; font-weight:normal ; line-height:17px ; " target= " blank " &gt; David Foster&lt;/a&gt; ( @davidfoster ) on &lt;time style= " font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; line-height:17px ; " datetime= " **25;5501;TOOLONG " &gt;Mar 17 , 2020 at 7:04pm **45;5528;TOOLONG ... &lt;script async src= " **28;5575;TOOLONG " &gt;&lt;/sc ... Mike Devlin , Victoria Times ColonistWed , 18 Mar 2020 20:25:04 **45;5605;TOOLONG ... things to know about Are You in Love , by Basia Bulathttp : **35;5652;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;5689;TOOLONG ... title= " Basia Bulat. " alt= " Basia Bulat. " data-portal-copyright= " RICHMOND LAM " **28;5725;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1237253 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Are You in Love&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Basia Bulat&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Secret City Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to believe that she was once known as an autoharp-strumming fresh face on the folk circuit . Because singer/songwriter Basia Bulat is now a full-on torch singer with serious pop sensibilities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her music has become a delightful hybrid of such &amp;rsquo ; 70s acts as &amp;mdash ; no , really &amp;mdash ; Captain &amp;amp ; Tennille blended with some cosmic southern soul and a dash of vintage new wave all thrown together to illuminate her instantly recognizable soaring vibrato. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Working once again with Jim James ( My Morning Jacket ) , who also helmed 2016&amp;rsquo ; s Good Advice , Bulat explores even denser textures and arrangements on the album&amp;rsquo ; s 13 tracks . Fans will be surprised &amp;mdash ; pleasantly &amp;mdash ; by the prog rock elements in songs such as Light Years , and it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to miss the arena-oriented feel of many of the songs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are five things to know about Are You in Love ? : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : Bowie influence&lt;/strong&gt; . Anyone listening to the title track , and album opener , is likely to get a sense of deja vu for David Bowie&amp;rsquo ; s classic Five Years from this song . It&amp;rsquo ; s got a similar ebb and flow in the drama of the delivery and one of only a few fist-in-the-air belters that Bulat has recorded . Her vocals are powerful throughout , but this song really was a good choice to introduce the album . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Your Girl&lt;/strong&gt; . Time to get your new wave on people . This bouncy tune has Cars-style keyboards , a totally defining bass line , and the surprisingly sad lyric is perfect juxtaposed against the happy music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Homesick&lt;/strong&gt; . This is classic Basia . Sparse strummed autoharp with just a smattering of what sounds almost like a toy piano accompanying her at first as she drops a vocal that could come right from a classic country tune . Then the whole thing blows up with a near wall of sound-style arrangement as she belts out &amp;ldquo ; I get homesick/Can you remember why&amp;rdquo ; to someone who &amp;mdash ; apparently &amp;mdash ; isn&amp;rsquo ; t listening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : No Control&lt;/strong&gt; . While the lead single is the poppy Your Girl , this funky mid-tempo ballad just might be a breakout track . The way the words &amp;lsquo ; no control&amp;rsquo ; are repeated , almost chant-like , just layers on the heartbreak while the backing band swings hard . By the end , it&amp;rsquo ; s exhilarating in the way that a soul ballad should be . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Love is at the End of the World&lt;/strong&gt; . It&amp;rsquo ; s also at the end of the album&amp;rsquo ; s lengthy listen , as this song closes out the set . Yet another piano-driven mid tempo ballad that goes for that almost trancelike repetition of the song title , this one stands out for its slow , percolating electric piano backing and one of Bulat&amp;rsquo ; s breathiest vocals . She is in full power mode on most of Are You in Love ? It&amp;rsquo ; s nice to hear her drop back a bit too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;5755;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;5791;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Also out this week : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;African Head Charge&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Drumming is a Language : 1990-2011 Reissues Box Se&lt;/strong&gt; t &lt;em&gt;On-U Sound&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If ever there was perfect music for meditating away the long days of social-distancing and quarantine ahead , it&amp;rsquo ; s the avant-dub reggae pioneered by this seminal U.K. unit . Containing four heavily expanded versions of classic studio releases &amp;mdash ; Songs of Praise ( 1990 ) , In Pursuit of Shashamane Land , Visions of a Psychedelic Africa ( 2005 ) and Voodoo of the Godsent ( 2011 ) &amp;mdash ; as well as an album of unreleased tracks from the 1980s ( Churchical Chant of the Iyabinghi ) , this five-CD box set captures the collaboration between percussionist Bonjo Iyabinghi Noah and producer Adrian Sherwood . Without question , this is some of the deepest and most spiritual dub reggae ever crafted with songs from One Destination to Stoned Age Man still packing meaning and punch . Absolutely essential listening for anyone with a love of grooves , electronic beats and chanting to Jah. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;5827;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;5863;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Avishai Cohen&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Big Vicious&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;ECM Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trumpeter Cohen formed this experimental electronic fusion ensemble in 2013 after immigrating to the U.S. from Israel . Featuring Uzi Ramirez ( guitar ) , Yonatan Albalak ( guitar , bass ) , Aviv Cohen and Ziv Ravitz ( drums , live sampling ) , the 11 tracks range from the atmospheric ambient jams ( Honey Fountain , Moonlight Sonata , The Things You Tell Me ) to surprisingly rocking ( King Kutner ) . It&amp;rsquo ; s the kind of crossover sound that acts like Nils Petter Molvaer succeeded with decades back and it still resonates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;5899;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;5935;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; **35;5971;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **39;6008;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Napalm Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is going to be a surprise to fans of Myrkur ( Amalie Bruun ) and her symphonic black metal . Not that the haunting and spooky sounds associated with the artist are absent on this new record , just that she has gone full-folky on this 12-track record . Droning hardanger fiddles , hand drums , choruses and glockenspiels are all brought together in a modern take on Scandinavian folk that is so near New Age Celtic-style music you could probably gift it to your Enya-loving relative or your Dead Can Dance-loving goth buddy and both would be delighted . It&amp;rsquo ; s quite beautiful and Fager Som En Ros should be used in an episode next season of The Witcher. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;6049;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;6085;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; **37;6121;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Violent Nature&lt;/strong&gt; **38;6160;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This Calgary crew plays classic old-school metal with clean , blues-tinged lead vocals , lots of harmonies and really big riffs . They can get into some more driving psychedelic sounds as the single Vanishing Light makes abundantly clear . But this is really the kind of rock that fans of classic U.K. metal in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s will be instantly taken with . Sounds really good turned-up really loud , which is always a good test . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;6200;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;6236;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;6272;TOOLONG " **36;6303;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;6341;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;6370;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynWed , 18 Mar 2020 18:10:54 **45;6401;TOOLONG ... composer Meredith bridges pop and classical worlds -- with a dash of British game show musichttp : **35;6448;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;6485;TOOLONG ... title= " ' I 'm trying to keep the spectrum as broad as possible , ' Anna Meredith says of her new album FIBS . ' I like having the extremes of loud and quiet and fast and slow . ' Meredith is scheduled to play the Fox Cabaret on March 23. " alt= " ' I 'm trying to keep the spectrum as broad as possible , ' Anna Meredith says of her new album FIBS . ' I like having the extremes of loud and quiet and fast and slow . ' Meredith is scheduled to play the Fox Cabaret on March 23. " data-portal-copyright= " Gem Harris " **28;6521;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1238231 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anna Meredith spent nearly 15 years composing for park benches , elevator lifts and prestigious classical music programs before releasing a couple of EPs and finally , in 2016 , a debut solo album . That record , Varmints , received accolades in the pop-music press for its mix of bold arrangements and mellifluous hooks . The classically trained half-Canadian ( on her dad&amp;rsquo ; s side ) musician , who grew up in Scotland but makes her home in London , is now on tour to support her second full-length album , FIBS . We talked to Meredith about composing music for other people , her own work and futuristic British game show themes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Where is the most unusual place you 've heard your music ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s a big high street discount shop in the U.K. that I would never have thought in a billion years would add my stuff to their playlist . Sometimes I&amp;rsquo ; ve been in there buying huge quantities of pants or socks and it&amp;rsquo ; s playing , which is always slightly unexpected . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Your CV includes music for Hong Kong park benches , a Manchester shopping centre lift and the resurfacing machine at the Somerset House ice rink . Do you seek these projects out ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Mostly they&amp;rsquo ; ve come to me . Sometimes it&amp;rsquo ; s been like , &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; d like you to write an installation piece , what do you fancy ? &amp;rdquo ; For example , I was given a choice of locations in the shopping centre . The idea came itself when I saw the lift and it made complete sense what I should do . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You also wrote the music for Bo Burnham 's coming-of-age comedy Eighth Grade and the Netflix Paul Rudd series Living With Yourself . What do people expect or want when they approach you about projects like these ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; With both of those things , the people who approached me seemed to know quite a bit of my stuff already . They said they wanted something quite strong , that had a real character . They didn&amp;rsquo ; t want background drones or cutesy mandolins , but something that would add tension or quite a lot of weight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;6551;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;6587;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What kind of work have you turned down ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; A whole bunch of stuff over the years , lots of orchestral work , film projects . Mostly because I had to carve out time to make the new album , nearly a year&amp;rsquo ; s worth of time . It involves a real leap of faith to turn down that paid work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : The new album jumps around a lot from genre to genre -- at one point I had to check to make sure it was still the same record . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m trying to keep the spectrum as broad as possible . I like having the extremes of loud and quiet and fast and slow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : At recent shows with your band ( Maddie Cutter on cello , Jack Ross on electric guitar , Tom Kelly on tuba and Sam Wilson on drums ) , you 've been performing a medley that includes songs by ABBA ( Lay All Your Love on Me ) , Carly Rae Jepsen ( Call Me Maybe ) , Daniel Bedingfield ( Got ta Get Thru This ) , and Moldovan pop group O-Zone ( Dragostea Din Tei ) . What does this say about your relationship to pop music ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I don&amp;rsquo ; t really listen to much music at all . The songs in our little encore medley are just things I liked once for whatever reason , I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t even tell you . Also , each one focuses on a different member of the band , so it reflects what they like as well . I listen more to audiobooks. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : There 's also a bit of the theme from a British game show called The Crystal Maze in there , is that right ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Yeah , it&amp;rsquo ; s just a tiny second of an in-joke . Even in the U.K. , it will pass most people by . But it&amp;rsquo ; s just this very amazing theme tune for this futuristic game show and is kind of bonkers . If you ever have a boring afternoon I&amp;rsquo ; d recommend watching old episodes . It&amp;rsquo ; s a big part of my childhood , so it felt like a nice way to wrap it all up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Anna Meredith was scheduled to perform at Fox Cabaret in Vancouver on March 23 . Email &lt;a href= " **28;7437;TOOLONG " **35;7467;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynTue , 17 Mar 2020 14:03:38 **45;7504;TOOLONG ... music : Vancouver Symphony Orchestra plays on to empty Orpheumhttp : **33;7551;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;7586;TOOLONG ... title= " Piano soloist Saleem Ashkar and VSO music director Otto Tausk , before the start of the performance of Beethoven 's Piano Concerto No. 4 in an empty Orpheum because of COVID-19 restrictions on March 15 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra " **28;7622;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1241395 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was not supposed to end like this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recently , the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra began an &lt;a href= " https : **33;7652;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;ambitious festival celebrating 250 years of Beethoven&lt;/a&gt; . But , after only a few events , the orchestra cancelled live performances for the foreseeable future . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was an understandable and wise decision . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After what must have been some intensely confusing days , the VSO made a bold call : To take two classic Beethoven works , the Fourth Piano Concerto and the Sixth Symphony and play them at the Orpheum March 15 as a livestream broadcast to interested listeners . ( View the concert at &lt;a href= " https : **33;7687;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;7722;TOOLONG ... ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7756;TOOLONG ... title= " Members of the VSO play to an empty Orpheum because of COVID-19 restrictions on March 15 , 2020. " alt= " Members of the VSO play to an empty Orpheum because of COVID-19 restrictions on March 15 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra " **28;7792;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1241393 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was invited to sit in rather splendid isolation in the empty theatre to watch . Downtown Vancouver was by no means deserted on this bright afternoon , but it certainly was subdued . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And although I&amp;rsquo ; m no stranger to the backstage world of classical music , it did feel decidedly odd to slink in by the stage door and wend my way through the Orpheum&amp;rsquo ; s unglamorous nether regions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The players were assembled in formal orchestra black . At 2 p.m. on the dot , maestro Otto Tausk was prepared to give the downbeat . The tech guys weren&amp;rsquo ; t quite ready , resulting in a few extra-long moments of pent-up tension . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was a unique experience and the orchestra members were well aware of its significance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , pianist Saleem Ashkar began the especially intimate opening measures of the concerto , a solo piano that was vulnerable in the emptiness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a few moments , it was time for the orchestra to join in and the familiar , though still magical , concerto was truly underway &amp;mdash ; though with a certain distracted fragility. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the recapitulation of the first movement , it was apparent that conductor , soloist and orchestra were all completely focused on the shared task of bringing Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s concerto to life . Circumstances were irrelevant . This was all about the music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;7822;TOOLONG ... title= " Members of the VSO play to an empty Orpheum. " alt= " Members of the VSO play to an empty Orpheum. " data-portal-copyright= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra " **28;7858;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1241394 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m a fan of Tausk&amp;rsquo ; s approach to classical-era repertoire , because he brings a 21st century awareness of early 19th century performance practices to his interpretations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To that end , he arranged the orchestra in a manner that Beethoven himself would have recognized : first violins on the left ; second violins on the right ; trumpets next to their percussion cousin , the timpani ; and a phalanx of seven double basses across the back of the stage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was no quest for novelty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There were immediate acoustic benefits from a rich , very present bass sound . For example , in the concerto&amp;rsquo ; s second movement &amp;mdash ; a sort of argument between a choleric orchestra and a more sanguine soloist &amp;mdash ; the empty hall added an extra measure of resonance to the orchestral outbursts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The reading of the Pastoral Symphony was no less persuasive , dozens of musicians playing with chamber music finesse and a truly collaborative spirit under the leadership of a fine conductor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A lot has happened in the last few weeks . In the great scheme of things , the cancellation of classical concerts is just a small part of our new , complicated reality . But as the performance unfolded , I realized that this was the first time that I had really considered the implications of how my life as a critic with a routine of two to three concerts a week has changed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was a sobering realization . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before the event , I had quick chats with both Tausk and the VSO&amp;rsquo ; s new chief administrator , Angela Elster . With this exceptional event out of the way , Tausk plans to fly back to his family in The Netherlands ; Elster has the unenviable responsibility of keeping the VSO&amp;rsquo ; s home fires burning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both were adamant about the power of the great classics to speak to us in difficult times . The entirely unanticipated finale to the Beethoven festival said loudly and clearly : &amp;ldquo ; This is who we are . This is what we do . This is what matters . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;7888;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7930;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;7966;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;7994;TOOLONG ... about the VSO&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;8030;TOOLONG ... our list of cancelled events **25;8066;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;8093;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? ArtsMusicVancouver arts community changes outlook on coronavirus impact : ' Everybody is suffering from this . Everybody'http : **30;8247;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8279;TOOLONG ... title= " ARCHIVE - Interior image of the Orpheum Theatre.The venue was set to be the location of several Vancouver Symphony Orchestra events in March , which have since been cancelled . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;8315;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1239371 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Delayed movie premieres , postponed international tours by Live Nation and AEG and an ever-growing list of cancelled events &amp;mdash ; the novel coronavirus has left the arts and entertainment industry at large scrambling to keep up with the ever-changing situation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And it&amp;rsquo ; s a similar state of affairs in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the word circulating within the local &lt;a href= " http : **34;8345;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;communities was one of cautious optimism just a few days prior&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; venues and representatives interviewed noted that &amp;ldquo ; additional measures&amp;rdquo ; were being taken to help limit concerns related to the spread of COVID-19 &amp;mdash ; most remained confident that the shows would , indeed , go on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , after a press conference with Health Minister Adrian Dix and Provincial Health Officer Dr. Bonnie Henry late Thursday introduced a ban on gatherings of over 250 people , it seemed only a matter of time before more cancellation announcements would be released . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And come , they did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; some organizers remained somewhat tight-lipped about the future of their events given the ever-changing nature of the health environment , preferring to continue with a wait-and-see approach , others , like Monster Jam , made postponement announcements that promised rescheduling at a later date . Several organizations and venues such as B.C. Place , the Growing Room Festival , Vancouver Civic Theatres , which operates the Orpheum , Queen Elizabeth Theatre , Vancouver Playhouse and Annex and the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra announced the outright cancellation of all forthcoming performances and events in light of COVID-19 during the month of March and on into April . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vancouver Recital Society was one of the first local organizations to release the cancellation information , doing so even before the announcement on the ban of large gatherings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve had at least a week of sleepless nights , &amp;rdquo ; Leila Getz , the founder and artistic director of the organization , says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s so beyond what any of us have ever had to cope with , to date . I mean , 9/11 was pretty shocking , but , I don&amp;rsquo ; t remember having felt quite as disrupted as now . And I think that&amp;rsquo ; s because it is like a snowball that is running down the hill , and you know that it&amp;rsquo ; s coming , but you&amp;rsquo ; re just waiting for it to come . And , there&amp;rsquo ; s an indecision about how to react or how fast to react . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The society&amp;rsquo ; s decision was to cancel all performance through the end of the month , citing the need to protect their audience at all costs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our audience , although we do have a number of younger people , our prime audience is older . And , they are the ones that I care about the most . They are the ones that have built trust in us over the years . They are almost like family , &amp;rdquo ; Getz says . &amp;nbsp ; &amp;ldquo ; God forbid one person should get sick at one of the concerts . So , that came first , before thinking about the money , and the artists who wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be able to travel , and all that sort of thing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Getz says finally making the decision , strangely enough , provided some relief during a week that has , for many , felt increasingly tense . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This may sound weird , but having made that decision , was like it took a huge weight off my shoulders . It felt like such a relief , &amp;rdquo ; Getz says . &amp;ldquo ; Because , it was the uncertainty of the last few weeks &amp;mdash ; and not knowing what to do . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The impact of cancellations will have an indelible impact on arts organizations large and small . But the effects on non-profit arts outfits , in particular , will be immense , insiders say . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These are unprecedented times . We are a non-profit charity and youth-serving organization , and the month of March is Carousel Theatre for Young People&amp;rsquo ; s second busiest month of the year , &amp;rdquo ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Carole Higgins , the a&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;rtistic and managing director for &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Carousel Theatre for Young People , said in a news release announcing the cancellation of the current production of The House at Pooh Corner . " &lt;/span&gt; The financial impact of the closure &amp;hellip ; is considerable . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the release , the theatre group asked for patrons to consider accepting a gift certificate for the value of the purchased ticket in lieu of a full refund to assist with &amp;ldquo ; cash flow&amp;rdquo ; during the time , as well as to consider taking a tax receipt as a donation to the organization . As of press time , the small-group spring break drama camps were slated to continue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Proactive measures and precautions are , without a doubt , paramount when it comes to improving the collective health outlook in an unprecedented situation such as this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t mean we can&amp;rsquo ; t hope ( if somewhat over-optimistically ) for an expeditious solution that will see the reduction of &amp;ldquo ; social distancing&amp;rdquo ; and a return to a full calendar of events within the city . Given the uncertainty of the situation , attempting to cobble together a timeline for how long the situation will continue &amp;mdash ; and continue to intensify &amp;mdash ; may seem foolhardy to some , but optimistic to others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Who can put a timeline on this ? Who can put a number on this ? I think that we&amp;rsquo ; ve just got to hope that all these strict measures that are now being put in place will help to kind of flatten it , &amp;rdquo ; Getz says . &amp;ldquo ; Everybody is suffering from this . Everybody . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I just hope that we can all survive this . We have to . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With this in mind , we made the decision to proceed with our annual spring arts preview , expanding the offering to include several events that stretch closer toward the summer season than simply to the spring . The dates and events are , without a doubt , subject to date change and cancellation . And , a &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;s the impact of COVID-19 continues to evolve in B.C. , it 's important to note that individuals should consider their personal situations when deciding whether or not to attend . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From new titles from local authors , to theatre events and music , here are the seasonal highlights that caught our writers&amp;rsquo ; imaginations &amp;mdash ; long before COVID-19 captured our collective reality . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;8381;TOOLONG " **35;8411;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Aleesha HarrisFri , 13 Mar 2020 20:33:27 **45;8448;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusic5 things to know about Bloom/Burn Out by Krystle Dos Santoshttp : **34;8495;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8531;TOOLONG ... title= " Krystle dos Santos plays the Imperial on March 21. " alt= " Krystle dos Santos plays the Imperial on March 21. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;8567;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1229856 " /&gt; **31;8597;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Krystle Dos Santos&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **27;8630;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;8659;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mixing her time between music and acting , Vancouver-based artist Krystle Dos Santos has been steadily building towards her new album since her self-titled Western Canadian Music Award-winning 2008 debut . That acclaimed recording was followed by the even better Fame Fatale , which also received a WCMA nomination . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bloom/Burn deserves much more attention than that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over 10 tracks , this Edmonton-raised Guyanese musician pays homage to the sounds of Sixties and Seventies soul , classic Motown , and deep rhythm and blues . Backed by her quintet , Dos Santos&amp;rsquo ; voice soars , roars and delivers stories of love and devotion that sound like instant classics . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there is no question that it&amp;rsquo ; s a hard row to hoe as a soul singer in Vancouver deserving national recognition , Dos Santos has what it takes to do it , as her past appearances at events such as the Honey Jam Toronto indicate . Here are five things to know about Bloom/Burn. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : Bloom . &lt;/strong&gt; The album opens with a horn section intro that could have been on a Stax label Otis Redding session . Then the voice comes in with maximum impact at a low , bluesy pace that fits the riff perfectly . At around the 50 second mark , she does a little jump on the verse about &amp;ldquo ; the line through the clouds&amp;rdquo ; that just raises the hair on the back of your neck . That&amp;rsquo ; s what you want in a song . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Here 4 U&lt;/strong&gt; . A stunner of a funk groove built off of electric piano , pulsing clean bass and a some very nimble guitar picking bubbles underneath this song . Then the jazziness is paired up with more blasting horns and some hip-shaking percussion to hit that sweet spot of being both understated but impossible to not want to move to . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;8691;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;8727;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Buried Alive&lt;/strong&gt; . It would be a mistake to classify Dos Santos as a neo-soul act . Although she does appear to have an affinity for the stylings of a different era , she is also most certainly working with modern urban pop motifs as well . Nowhere is this better demonstrated than on this arena-sized tune . From its gigantic drum break to its fist-punching whoa-oh&amp;rsquo ; s , this is more Rihanna or Beyonc&amp;eacute ; than Aretha . Simply put , this is an artist who appears able to do it all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : I Cry&lt;/strong&gt; . With it&amp;rsquo ; s reggae-rock rhythm and heavy arrangement , this is unlike any other piece on Bloom/Burn . It also demonstrates yet another side of Dos Santos&amp;rsquo ; considerable range . It&amp;rsquo ; s no surprise that she has been in so many musical theatre performances ranging from the Arts Club Theatre&amp;rsquo ; s Dream Girls to the Firehall Arts Centre&amp;rsquo ; s The Chelsea Hotel : The Songs of Leonard Cohen . The woman can deliver dramatically . She is working on a new cabaret musical titled Hey Viola ! about Canadian civil rights activist Viola Desmond with The Chelsea Hotel writer Tracey Power ( Miss Shakespeare , Glory ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Walk On Through&lt;/strong&gt; . OK , this is an obvious single to release from the record . A very Northern Soul-style rave-up with a chorus somewhat reminiscent of Spice Girls&amp;rsquo ; Stop . It&amp;rsquo ; s bold and brassy statement that can become something of a signature . You just know this is a killer in concert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Also out this week : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;8763;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;8799;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Anna Calvi&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **35;8835;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Domino Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Choosing to make her latest album a reworking of seven tracks from her acclaimed 2018 Mercury Prize-nominated release , Hunted seems a bit odd at first . But one need only listen to Calvi and Julia Holter&amp;rsquo ; s approach to Swimming Pool to appreciate just how utterly new the music is . This chorally orchestrated track is a standout , but so are the ethereal Eden ( feat . Charlotte Gainsburg ) , snarky Don&amp;rsquo ; t Beat the Girl Out of My Boy ( feat . Courtney Barnett ) , and others . Calvi&amp;rsquo ; s reframing of Indies or Paradise into something that sounds like it could have come off of PJ Harvey&amp;rsquo ; s Dry is fantastic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;8872;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;8908;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Chats&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;High Risk Behaviour&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Dine Alone Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From singer/guitarist Eamon Sandwith&amp;rsquo ; s tragic bowl cut/mullet hairstyle to having the viral video Succulent Chinese Meal Guy star in their Dine N Dash video , every thing that seems beyond stupid about this Sunshine Coast , Queensland , Australia punk trio probably isn&amp;rsquo ; t . Formed when the three members were in high school , the band&amp;rsquo ; s brand of young , loud and snotty music is delightfully cheeky , relentlessly entertaining and it&amp;rsquo ; s not hard to see why the group&amp;rsquo ; s odes to smoke breaks ( Smoko ) and local haunts ( Ross River ) and everyday Aussie occurrences ( The Clap , Heatstroke ) is killer . A hot album with those in the know for a year or more , it&amp;rsquo ; s now available in North America , doubtless in advance of a tour . Yay . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;8944;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;8980;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jonathan Wilson&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Dixie Blu&lt;/strong&gt; r &lt;em&gt;BMG/Bella Union&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Playing David Gilmore to Roger Waters on Waters&amp;rsquo ; Us + Them tour left Wilson with some new tricks to approaching his easy going Seventies Laurel Canyon-era style songwriting . The opening of Just For Love has a keyboard passage that could be straight out of Shine On You Crazy Diamond , and his love of reverb-drenched pedal steel across the album echoes Pink Floyd guitar , too . But what really stands out is the many different vocal styles Wilson employs from a gentle whisper tone ( O&amp;rsquo ; Girl ) and a ringing country twang ( &amp;rsquo ; 69 Corvette ) to drawling roots rocker ( Enemies ) . At 14 songs , it&amp;rsquo ; s a bit long . But the songs all stand up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;9016;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;9052;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Spectres&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **38;9088;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Artoffact Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brian Gustavson&amp;rsquo ; s post-punk crew really knows how to conjure up the moody atmospherics of 1980s UK bands . The most obvious influence would have to be Joy Division/New Order , as the opening track The Head and the Heart makes clear . Over the course of the nine tracks , the group offers up everything from the addictive drive of When Possessed Pray to the downright dance-happy Fate . The production by ACTORS&amp;rsquo ; Jason Corbett deserves mention as it just crackles through the speakers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;9128;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9170;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;9206;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9237;TOOLONG ... latest music **25;9273;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;9300;TOOLONG " **36;9331;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;9369;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;9398;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 13 Mar 2020 20:05:25 **45;9429;TOOLONG ... Riot is more a movement than a musical acthttp : **37;9476;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;9515;TOOLONG ... title= " Pussy Riot live in 2018. " alt= " Pussy Riot live in 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " IGNACIO ORREGO @FOTOROCK " **28;9551;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1229827 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Pussy Riot&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Cancelled &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Fortune Sound Club , 147 E. Pender St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; $26 at Red Cat Records , Zulu and &lt;a href= " https : **33;9581;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;9616;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Rally with us in 2020 ! We encourage you to come to the performances with posters and banners , and with your face covered -- masks , balaclavas , scarfs , anything . Let 's riot ! &lt;/em&gt; &amp;mdash ; Pussy Riot Team &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; According to Nadezhda Tolokonnikova , Pussy Riot is a political movement first and a band after that . This poses the question of what exactly people are paying to see when whatever membership of the Russian anarcha-feminist collective steps on the Fortune Sound Club stage next week ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The co-founder of the Moscow-based agitprop collective explains it like this : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Music , obviously , but Pussy Riot came about as a movement for political statement and action first , &amp;rdquo ; said Tolokonnikova. &amp;ldquo ; We would stage unsanctioned performances with whoever was available to participate and the music was a vehicle to add impact . It was often samples of other bands mixed together , but as things have developed , the band has become a vehicle for presenting ideas with a somewhat more fixed membership and focus on the songs . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Formed in 2011 by Yekaterina Samutsevich , Tolokonnikova and her ex-husband Pyotr Verzilov , Pussy Riot has its origins in the anarchist art collective Voina , known for political and provocative **26;9644;TOOLONG actions . Made up of art-school students , academic activists and more , Voina mounted numerous art &amp;ldquo ; actions&amp;rdquo ; including a particularly memorable one on June 14 , 2010. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Titled Dick Captured by the FSB , the event included painting a 65-metre-long phallus on the surface of the Liteyny drawbridge leading to the headquarters of the Federal Security Service in St. Petersburg . The painting , titled Giant Galactic Space Penis , delivered a probing message to all who could see it when the drawbridge was raised . Anyone who thinks that there isn&amp;rsquo ; t radicalism in Russia would do well to read up on the past activities of groups such as this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The need to separate Russia and the Russian people from Putin , from the government , is so important , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; There are people actively opposed to the state putting themselves at great risk there like there are everywhere , and so many of the issues are the same as in the rest of the world . Pussy Riot is about getting together and doing something about it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pussy Riot formed out of the Moscow faction of Voina . From its beginning , the use of web-based technology was key to creating its enduring statements and spreading them throughout Russia and beyond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The group staged impromptu &amp;ldquo ; concerts&amp;rdquo ; at various sites in the city , typically dressed in loudly coloured dresses and leggings , wearing balaclavas and conducting interviews under pseudonyms . These events were intended to raise awareness around issues dear to the membership , which ranged from feminism and LGBTQ rights to social inequality and prisoner&amp;rsquo ; s rights . Underlying all the group&amp;rsquo ; s work is fierce opposition to autocratic and kleptocratic rule , particularly that of Russian President Vladimir Putin. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Feb. 21 , 2012 , Pussy Riot catapulted into the international spotlight when they performed for 35 seconds inside Moscow&amp;rsquo ; s Cathedral of Christ the Saviour to create a video clip for the song Punk Prayer : Mother of God Drive Putin Away . This performance led to the later arrests of Maria Alyokhina and Tolokonnikova , who were charged with hooliganism and imprisoned without bail . Samutsevich was also arrested in conjunction with the event . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;All three were sentenced to two years in a penal colony , with Samutsevich lucky to get released on appeal . Tolokonnikova says it was a terrible experience , but it did n't lessen her progressive convictions . Upon release from prison in Krasnoyarsk , she flashed a victory sign to journalists , shouting " Russia without Putin. " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Prison in Russia is part of the program to oppress people and conditions are so bad , so brutal , that it&amp;rsquo ; s understandable why everyone is scared to do anything that could draw the attention of the authorities and put you at risk , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; I still have nightmares about being put back in prison . But you have no choice but to oppose what is happening , not only in Russia . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The conviction generated international outrage and the three women became the focus of attention from organizations ranging from Amnesty International to the German Bundestag . Even Hillary Clinton voiced support . By the time they were released from prison , Alyokhina and Tolokonnikova were global celebrities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After their prison release , books such as Words Will Break Cement : The Passion of Pussy Riot ( Riverside Press ) and documentaries such as director Mike Lerner&amp;rsquo ; s and Maxim Pozdorovkin&amp;rsquo ; s Pussy Riot : A Punk Prayer put the members in the spotlight . The timing couldn&amp;rsquo ; t have been better for spreading the Pussy Riot message . The putsch against Western democratic values taking place in Europe , the U.K. and the U.S. begged for a continued , international-level stance against state corruption , police brutality , prison conditions and the attack on women , immigrant , minority and LGBTQ rights . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The group certainly nailed the outcome of the 2016 American election , releasing its frightfully prophetic video titled Make America Great Again only a week before Donald Trump&amp;rsquo ; s triumphant presidential bid . Opening with Tolokonnikova acting as an anchor on &amp;ldquo ; Trump TV&amp;rdquo ; ( which now exists ) proclaiming that &amp;ldquo ; everyone&amp;rsquo ; s favourite , the great Donald Trump , has won the presidential election , &amp;rdquo ; the song then attacks and subverts many of the MAGA hatters&amp;rsquo ; favourite quotes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Did I wish that we had been wrong about that , yes , definitely , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; But there are so many similarities between Putin and Trump and the people behind them , that it&amp;rsquo ; s not that surprising . There is a strong sentiment in a certain part of society that the past was better , that we need to go back to the &amp;lsquo ; good old days . &amp;rsquo ; Somehow , this is going to fix the problems in the world ; it will not . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So Pussy Riot will be on the road doing its part &amp;mdash ; the band announced the tour would be postponed due to coronavirus concerns &amp;mdash ; playing its grinding electronic noise/punk songs such as the self-explanatory Police State and I Can&amp;rsquo ; t Breathe , written in honour of Eric Garner , who died after NYPD officer Daniel Pantaleo put him in a chokehold during an arrest . They have also dropped the fierce track Hangerz ( featuring Vic Mensa and Junglepussy ) , which challenges the continuing attacks against women&amp;rsquo ; s reproductive choice and rights around the world . Pussy Riot had plans to partner with PLUS1 to put $1 of every ticket sold to support &lt;a href= " https : **26;9672;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Action Canada for Sexual Health and Rights&lt;/a&gt; ( formerly the Planned Parenthood Federation of Canada ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tolokonnikova notes in a news release : &amp;ldquo ; Stop banning and punishing me . Treat me as truly equal . Talk to me . Listen to me . Help me when I need it . Planned parenthood and their affiliates are places where women can receive real support , and we , the women of Pussy Riot , cherish these unique safe places dearly . And we&amp;rsquo ; re seriously dedicated to continuously support them . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Get the latest updates on &lt;a href= " https : **33;9700;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Pussy Riot&lt;/a&gt; news on Facebook and the &lt;a href= " https : **33;9735;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;9770;TOOLONG station on YouTube. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;9799;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;9835;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;9871;TOOLONG " **36;9902;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;9940;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;9969;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 13 Mar 2020 19:23:52 **45;10000;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " KAMIL KRZACZYNSKI " **28;10120;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223837 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If 2019 seemed like the year of farewell tours , then 2020 is leaning toward greeting rising stars . That&amp;rsquo ; s not to say there aren&amp;rsquo ; t big name classic rockers on the road &amp;mdash ; The Rolling Stones fit that bill better than any other act on the planet &amp;mdash ; but there appears to be plenty of new talent that can pack an arena , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As far as exciting new music goes , the decade is off to a good start . That so many emerging artists also happen to be women in a male-dominated industry is one more bonus . After growing up having to listen to how their granddads couldn&amp;rsquo ; t get no satisfaction , young women are demanding some now . It makes for more interesting music lyrically and emotionally than has been heard since the post-punk era saw a huge influx of all-female or female-fronted groups releasing rousing records . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After years of radio churning up variations of whining grunge , post-grunge , n&amp;uuml ; grunge , retro grunge bands , it&amp;rsquo ; s awesome to have something fresh sounding out . Prime examples of exciting sounds include Grammy ruler Billie Eilish , whose Vancouver show has been sold out for months , and Polaris Prize-winner Haviah Mighty , who is here for a limited club engagement . Many others will be following . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are three must-see shows coming in 2020 : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;10150;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10186;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Amyl and the Sniffers&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;10216;TOOLONG : April 13 , 7 p.m. ( subject to change ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;10251;TOOLONG : The Rickshaw Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : $20 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;10287;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;10322;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s probably not cool to say , but the Aussie music scene is on fire these days . And if your taste runs towards gritty pub rock with classic punk influences , then Amyl and the Sniffers is a quartet you need to check out . Fronted by Amy Taylor , the group&amp;rsquo ; s sound mixes in elements of late &amp;rsquo ; 60s Detroit gutter rock , swaggering glammy &amp;rsquo ; 70s punk , and some decidedly Antipodean pub boogie . It&amp;rsquo ; s not that surprising the music is raw , given that the band&amp;rsquo ; s name is a play on local slang for street level club drug amyl nitrate . It&amp;rsquo ; s loud , brash and more than a little bit hooligan , right down to those tragic London bike courier haircuts some of the band members are sporting . The band&amp;rsquo ; s last show in town at the Fox Cabaret is still buzzed about . To put what that means in context , that represents 58 years of kicking out the jams in the same band for Mick Jagger , Keith Richards and Charlie Watts . NewsMusicFive shows in Vancouver to see over spring breakhttp : **35;11695;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;11732;TOOLONG ... title= " Abbath is a Bergen , Norway , black metal band fronted by lead singer and guitarist Abbath Doom Occulta , front . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11768;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230442 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A visit to the Vancouver school board &lt;a href= " https : **33;11798;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;website&lt;/a&gt; declares spring break is March 16-27 . That means different things to different folks . Students of all ages revel in the respite from routine . Parents recoil in terror of what to do with all of them running free once more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Keeping the kids of all ages busy during the break is big business . Believe it or not , a likely majority won&amp;rsquo ; t be taking the time off to get caught up on coursework that could perhaps use some attention . The main assignment is to have fun . But what kind ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new of the day isn&amp;rsquo ; t conducive to time-tested , spring break time fillers . Mounting concerns over the coronavirus find a lot of travel getting nixed . From camps , slopes and beyond , recreation and risk appear to be in a joust for dominance . But you can rest assured that artists will still be touring . It takes a lot more than a potential pandemic to keep musicians from money , and fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With that in mind , here is a mix of five concerts that looked well worth attending over the next 11 days . Just remember to get a lot of rest the day after you spend the night out raging to keep your energy up . ( Not a real ) doctor&amp;rsquo ; s orders . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;11833;TOOLONG ... title= " Tamino . " alt= " Tamino . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11869;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230496 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : Tamino&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 16 , 7 p.m. ( subject to change ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Fox Cabaret &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : $15 at Red Cat Record , Zulu and &lt;a href= " https : **33;11899;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;11934;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2018 The Independent wondered if this Belgian-Egyptian model and musician was the heir to Jeff Buckley . That is a pretty grand comparison , but Tamino-Amir Moharam Fouad&amp;rsquo ; s soaring falsetto certainly has attracted a host of global supporters , ranging from Live Nation to members of Radiohead . His 2018 debut full-length album Amir was licensed by taste-making Canadian indie label Arts &amp;amp ; Crafts , and now he&amp;rsquo ; s strumming to our shores . Given both his considerable following in the Low Countries and modelling features in the March 2019 Vogue Hommes , you won&amp;rsquo ; t be seeing him in venues the size of the Fox Cabaret much longer . Did we also mention that he&amp;rsquo ; s the grandson of Egyptian singer and film star Muharram Fouad ? Well , now we have . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;11962;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;11998;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;12034;TOOLONG ... title= " Norwegian musician Abbath ( Olve Eikemo ) performs on Day 2 of the Heavy Montreal music festival at Jean-Drapeau park on Aug. 8 , 2015. " data-portal-copyright= " Dario Ayala " **28;12070;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230490 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Decibel Tour : Abbath , The True Mayhem , Gatekeeper and Idle Hands&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 18 , 6 p.m. ( subject to change ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Imperial Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : $35 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;12100;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;12135;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Olve Eikemo , a.k.a . Abbath Doom Oculta or Abbath , is a legend in black metal . The Norwegian guitarist and singer first came to prominence in the influential act Immortal , in which he held down various positions ranging from vocalist to guitarist , drummer , keyboardist , lead vocalist and bassist . In 2016 , he released the first album by his new , self-titled band and has been blazing a trail of noisy mayhem ever since . From the corpse paint to the spiky leather gear , this is the kind of metal band that has fans singing &amp;ldquo ; don&amp;rsquo ; t go changin&amp;rsquo ; &amp;mdash ; ever . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;12164;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;12199;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;12235;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto pop artist Grandson . " alt= " Toronto pop artist Grandson . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;12271;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1230443 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Grandson&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 18 , 8 p.m. ( subject to change ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Commodore Ballroom , 868 Granville St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; From $36 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;12301;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;12336;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Toronto-based singer/songwriter Jordan Edward Benjamin has come a long way since his 2016 singles such as Bills and Things Change . As the title of his 2018 debut Fueled by Ramen EP suggests , A Modern Tragedy , Vol. 1 , isn&amp;rsquo ; t loaded up with chirpy pop ditties that make you wan na dance . The subject matter in songs can range from police brutality ( six minutes ) to gun violence ( Thoughts &amp;amp ; Prayers ) and the self-explanatory Rock Bottom . Having completed the trilogy of A Modern Tragedy , Vol 1-3 in 2019 , he&amp;rsquo ; s hitting the road in 2020 to spread the word about his &lt;a href= " https : //www.xxresistance.org/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;XX Resistance Fund&lt;/a&gt; . I can&amp;rsquo ; t offer any statistics , but once again this year March proffers an early spring bouquet of fine pianists . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Consider Saleem Ashkar , artistic director of the Galilee Chamber , who will be part of the VSO&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;13083;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Beethovenfest **25;13118;TOOLONG &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; featured in the sublime Fourth Piano Concerto and the oft maligned Choral Fantasy in programs March 13 and 14 . The extra incentive here is that Ritter won the first Chopin Competition on Period Instruments in Warsaw in 2018 . He&amp;rsquo ; ll be playing lots of Chopin , but also Beethoven , Haydn , and two far more obscure figures from the generation before Chopin : Franciszek Lessel ( 1780&amp;mdash ; 1838 ) , and Jan V&amp;aacute ; clav Hugo Vor&amp;iacute ; &amp;scaron ; ek ( 1791&amp;mdash ; 1825 ) . " alt= " The one , the only Andr ? s Schiff . Photo credit : Joanna Bergin. " data-portal-copyright= " Joanna Bergin " **28;13210;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1233431 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then there are solo recitals by &lt;strong&gt;Sir Andr ? s Schiff&lt;/strong&gt; and &lt;strong&gt;Benjamin Grosvenor&lt;/strong&gt; for the Vancouver Recital Society . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Schiff needs no introduction ; lucky for us , this performing giant of our age seems comfortable playing for Vancouver audiences . Over his regular appearances , he&amp;rsquo ; s given our audiences things that don&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily appear on the programs of other mid-sized towns . For example , we were the only place in Canada that got to hear his multi-concert Last Sonatas project , and before that we heard him do the entire first book of J.S. Bach&amp;rsquo ; s Well-Tempered Clavier. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For this visit Schiff focuses on Beethoven , six late middle period sonatas &lt;em&gt; ( March 24 at the Vancouver Playhouse ) &lt;/em&gt; . And two days earlier there&amp;rsquo ; s a special Vancouver Recital Society 40th birthday program at the Chan . It&amp;rsquo ; s an all-Bach program , with the Overture in the French Style and the Italian Concerto . Then ( fanfare , please ! ) the Goldberg Variations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canadians take something of a proprietary interest in Bach&amp;rsquo ; s mega-masterwork . While Glenn Gould didn&amp;rsquo ; t exactly put it on the map , he did bring it widespread popularity . And now every player , young or old , famous or not , reveres it as one of the commanding landmarks of the keyboard repertoire , and wants to show what they can bring to its performance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Schiff&amp;rsquo ; s Well Tempered Clavier back in 2012 still resonates with me as one of the great performances in my reviewing career . I fully anticipate his reading of the Goldbergs will pack a similar punch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A ( very ) young Benjamin Grosvenor made his Canadian debut for the recital society back in 2013 . Still in his 20s , he&amp;rsquo ; s pushing boundaries as he seeks to carve out his own niche in the crowded world of piano players by demonstrating both personal taste and sustained musical growth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Grosvenor&amp;rsquo ; s matinee on March 15 starts off with a keyboard work by Jean-Philippe Rameau . When was the last time we heard Rameau other than as an encore ? Grosvenor follows this with Schumann&amp;rsquo ; s Kreisleriana , an anthology of characteristic pieces that gives pianists plenty of scope for interpretive variety . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Grosvenor ends with another keyboard work : Liszt&amp;rsquo ; s gigantic , four-movements-in-one Sonata in B minor . Though Schumann and Liszt don&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily seem like the most complementary pair of composers , Grosvenor&amp;rsquo ; s done his homework : Liszt dedicated his exceptional , extravagant opus to Schumann &amp;mdash ; a quid pro quo for Schumann dedicating his Fantasy , Op. 17 to the 19th century&amp;rsquo ; s greatest keyboard showman a few years earlier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s going to be intense , but what month&amp;rsquo ; s worth of music ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13240;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13282;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;13318;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13346;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? " data-portal-copyright= " Steve Rose " **28;13634;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1231280 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;A Bowie Celebration&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 13 , 8 p.m. ( subject to change ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Commodore Ballroom ( over 19 only ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $72.25 ( inc. fees and taxes ) at &lt;a href= " https : **33;13664;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;13699;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; A roster of David Bowie alumni performs the entirety of the albums The Rise and Fall of Ziggy Stardust and the Spiders from Mars , and Diamond Dogs . Special guest vocalists include Sass Jordan , Joe Sumner , and Corey Glover ( Living Colour ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brooklyn-born bandleader/keyboardist Mike Garson has been touring with A Bowie Celebration for four years . Postmedia News talked to him about Chris Hadfield , Evan Rachel Wood , and doing songs that even Bowie never performed live . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You 've played all over the world and had over 100 guest vocalists sit in . How much rehearsal time do they get ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; We usually just get a sound check with them . They do their homework , I send them the music , we talk about the keys and the songs . It&amp;rsquo ; s really shooting from the hip . But it&amp;rsquo ; s exciting because usually they&amp;rsquo ; re great singers . Over the last four years , we&amp;rsquo ; ve had everyone from Sting to Lorde to Perry Farrell to Simon Le Bon to Joe Elliott . It&amp;rsquo ; s endless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : There 's at least one Canadian connection with Sass Jordan . Are there any other Canadian connections ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; In Toronto , we might have Commander ( Chris ) Hadfield the astronaut sit in . He loves to sing Bowie music . He did it last year and the first year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Actor Evan Rachel Wood has performed with the band as well . How did that come about ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; She&amp;rsquo ; s a good friend of mine , and she&amp;rsquo ; s sung with us two or three times over the last four years . This year she had a premiere of ( the HBO series ) Westworld so she couldn&amp;rsquo ; t be with us . But she&amp;rsquo ; s a gigantic Bowie fan . I&amp;rsquo ; ve recorded things with her , just myself on piano and her on voice . She loves David , she loves rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll and she can really put over a song . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What are her go-to tracks ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; She likes Moonage Daydream , she likes Rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; Roll Suicide . We did some esoteric stuff last year , like Slip Away ( from 2002&amp;rsquo ; s Heathen ) , which we recorded in my studio . She knows a lot of the music . She really grew up with it . She has the symbol from the Aladdin Sane album on her thigh , she had that done when she was 13. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Why did you decide to do these two albums in particular ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Normally I just do hits and deep covers . This is the first year where I&amp;rsquo ; m doing a concept &amp;mdash ; Diamond Dogs for the first part and Ziggy for the second part . I chose Diamond Dogs because it never got its due as a complete album . Some of the songs have never been sung , even with David . I was in the studio with him when we recorded it in 1974 at Olympic Studio , but some of those songs never saw the light of day in performance . So doing those songs is really exciting . And with Ziggy , there are so many famous songs on there , like Suffragette City , but there are obscure ones like Soul Love and Star and It Ain&amp;rsquo ; t Easy , and they sound amazing . In the over 40 years I worked with David , he never sang those songs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What do you want people to get out of A Bowie Celebration ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; You know , what I noticed &amp;mdash ; even last night on the first show of the American tour &amp;mdash ; is that I look out there and there are three generations . There are people who saw me in &amp;lsquo ; 72 with David , and they have their children , and their children are there . And they&amp;rsquo ; re singing the words to every song . And they leave with great memories , because David was a great songwriter ; they&amp;rsquo ; re wonderful songs , they inspire people . I want them to leave not only having had a great time but that it remains in their hearts their whole life . That&amp;rsquo ; s really why I&amp;rsquo ; m doing it . We&amp;rsquo ; re paying it forward . I was sort of the closest to David , having been with him so much , and I have such respect for his music , that I feel it has to go on forever . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13730;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;13772;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;14510;TOOLONG " **35;14540;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Harrison MooneyFri , 06 Mar 2020 02:08:48 **45;14577;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicClassical music : Discover the unsung women of Italian baroquehttp : **33;14624;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;14659;TOOLONG ... title= " Elektra Women 's Choir artistic director Morna Edmundson. " alt= " Elektra Women 's Choir artistic director Morna Edmundson. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;14695;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1227924 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Women of the Italian Baroque&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 7 , 7:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Pacific Spirit United Church &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;14725;TOOLONG : March 8 , 3 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Grosvenor Theatre , Kay Meek Arts Centre , &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;West Vancouver&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //elektra.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;elektra.ca &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Can we talk about our much overdue consideration of women composers ? Elektra Women&amp;rsquo ; s Choir offers a striking opportunity to sample music by seven composers in an impressive program with the Pacific baroque Orchestra . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14760;TOOLONG ... title= " Alexander Weimann. " alt= " Alexander Weimann. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;14796;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1227922 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A collaboration by one of North America&amp;rsquo ; s pre-eminent women&amp;rsquo ; s voice ensembles , Elektra Women&amp;rsquo ; s Choir , guest conductor Alexander Weimann , and the authentic instrument/period performance practice musicians of the Pacific baroque Orchestra would be an event in itself . As a showcase for forgotten talents from the baroque era , it&amp;rsquo ; s something very special . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s always a whiff of affirmative action to 21st century events of this sort ; and each time all sorts of misguided objections are trotted out . High time to try to think it all through . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First of all , there&amp;rsquo ; s the notion that if this music was any good , surely we would have heard it before . Not necessarily . Music history is fundamentally a 19th century concept . And the almost exclusively male musicologists who established the canon reflected the social attitudes of their age . They weren&amp;rsquo ; t out to deliberately exclude composers who were women ; but neither were they inclined to give women who composed much attention . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As this project amply demonstrates , lots of women were active in the baroque era . Many of the composers on the Elektra/PBO program worked in convents , unsung except in their particular communities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even a cursory contemplation of the seven life stories represented on the program gives us much to consider . Most came from privileged backgrounds , and received high-quality private musical education . Given the status of Italian music making in that era , this meant access to exceptional training . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But privilege came with a price . Women from this segment of society expected to be married off , complete with dowry . If an acceptable match could not be made , life in a nunnery was seen as an acceptable option , religious vocation or not . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Convent life for those with musical aptitude and compositional talent had perks : ready performance opportunities &amp;mdash ; at least within their communities &amp;mdash ; and , presumably , some free time to create . Plus their music was often preserved in convent libraries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The idea of career building in any modern sense was not remotely an option . Even so , most of the women to be heard on the program did publish music , which presupposed professional stature and a measure of general interest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At this moment in time we are very much in the early stages of a more inclusive rewrite of music history . To begin with , treasures had to be rooted out and modern performing editions created . Much of that work has been done in recent decades . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Next come projects like the Elektra/PBO collaboration : informed modern performers need to learn and to program these forgotten works . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last and most significantly , contemporary audiences need to hear the pieces . As we find out what we&amp;rsquo ; ve missed out on , we can then distinguish the exceptional from the remarkable . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m going to bet that with Alexander Weimann&amp;rsquo ; s instinct for real quality and Elektra&amp;rsquo ; s artistic director Morna Edmundson&amp;rsquo ; s rigorous performing standards , Women Composers of the Italian baroque should win passionate advocates for these forgotten women of the 17th and 18th centuries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It just a shame we&amp;rsquo ; ve all had to wait so long . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;14826;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;14868;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;14904;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;14932;TOOLONG ... for something to do ?
