ArtsMusicFive things to know about Ordinary Man by Ozzy Osbournehttp : **32;15153;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15187;TOOLONG ... title= " Ozzy Osbourne 's 12th solo album , Ordinary Man , has topped the U.S. iTunes rock chart , hit the top 10 on the Billboard album chart and is on its way to being his highest charting solo release in the U.K. ever . " alt= " Ozzy Osbourne 's 12th solo album , Ordinary Man , has topped the U.S. iTunes rock chart , hit the top 10 on the Billboard album chart and is on its way to being his highest charting solo release in the U.K. ever . " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Winter " **28;15223;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1224654 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Ordinary Man&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ozzy Osbourne&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Epic Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ozzy Osbourne&amp;rsquo ; s last solo album was 2010&amp;rsquo ; s Scream . But the crown prince of heavy metal has kept busy the past decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The singer toured one last time with Black Sabbath in support of the legendary Birmingham quartet&amp;rsquo ; s final album , 13 . Then he announced his second farewell tour , No More Tours II , in 2017 . Dates on that tour had to be postponed due to unspecific illness . Following a public announcement of his diagnosis of Parkinson&amp;rsquo ; s disease , the singer cancelled the tour and is off to Switzerland for six to eight weeks of specialized treatments . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ozzy aims to revive the tour in the U.K. in October . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s hoping he is able to give the fans one more go-around . His 12th solo album , Ordinary Man , has topped the U.S. iTunes rock chart , hit the top 10 on the Billboard album chart and is on its way to being his highest charting solo release in the U.K. ever . The Andrew Watt-produced album follows on a number of very well-received singles &amp;mdash ; Under the Graveyard , Straight to Hell , Ordinary Man ( f . Elton John ) and It&amp;rsquo ; s A Raid ( f . Post Malone ) &amp;mdash ; and received the best reviews he had since the Blizzard of Oz days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Without a doubt , the guest-artist laden 11-track record is one of the most varied and , frankly , honest of the Birmingham musician&amp;rsquo ; s career . With input from Red Hot Chili Peppers&amp;rsquo ; Chad Smith , Guns N Roses&amp;rsquo ; Duff McKagan , Rage Against the Machine&amp;rsquo ; s Tom Morello and others , the album is still the usual mixed bag that fans have come to expect . It&amp;rsquo ; s not equal to the best of Black Sabbath , but what is ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are five things to know about Ordinary Man : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 . Straight to Hell : &lt;/strong&gt; A warning to not &amp;ldquo ; take the hit&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; fly higher than a kite tonight&amp;rdquo ; from someone who survived what would have terminated most of us . The riff is super heavy , the drum into smashing and when Ozzy gives one of his signature &amp;ldquo ; alright now&amp;rdquo ; shout-outs , you&amp;rsquo ; ve got all the right elements . That said , while it may be woefully accurate , the verse about I&amp;rsquo ; ll make you scream/I&amp;rsquo ; ll make you defecate is just too funny . It conjures up images that are far more geriatric than demonic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 . Goodbye : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; There is no shortage of deliberate homages to past glories tossed into the tunes . If the booming bass drum and mechanized voice at the start of this one don&amp;rsquo ; t have you thinking Iron Man , the tempo jump around the halfway mark should . Producer Watt&amp;rsquo ; s soloing in this one is possibly the best of the entire album . Total head-banging sludge. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;15253;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;15289;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 . Ordinary Man : &lt;/strong&gt; Ozzy ballads haven&amp;rsquo ; t really been a highlight . But this declaration of how a ne&amp;rsquo ; er-do-well from a rough and tumble Northern English town reached pinnacles of fame and did it all for rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll works surprisingly well . Elton John&amp;rsquo ; s strong vocal turn has a lot to do with this and I suspect those Rocket Man-ish choruses came courtesy of the celebrated songwriter . Make no doubt about it , in concert this will put the phones up in the air as everyone gives a massive arena wave song . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 . Scary Little Green Men : &lt;/strong&gt; Without question , the best song title on Ordinary Man . Plus , with lyrics like &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re hiding amongst us , **25;15325;TOOLONG ; re silent/They may be violent/I don&amp;rsquo ; t care/How long have they been living in my head ? &amp;rdquo ; this is certain to be a classic . Morello contributes some pretty searing guitar and there is a McKagan bass riff similar to that one in the Welcome to the Jungle . You can&amp;rsquo ; t go wrong with that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 . It 's a Raid : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; The fastest song on the album is also one of the best . Post Malone is not the first person most would expect to drop a fierce metal/punk vocal , but here it is . The album could have used about three more songs at this tempo to break up what can get a bit plodding in the middle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Also reviewed : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;15352;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;15388;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;Jos ? James&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;No Beginning No End 2&lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;em&gt;Rainbow Blonde Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 10th release from this talented neo-soul singer is one of his slinkiest and most genre-slippery that he has released since 2013&amp;rsquo ; s No Beginning No End . Hence the title , and the presence of such top-tier collaborators as Ledisi and Christian Scott on the fantastic opener I Need You Love . James is such as smooth and natural singer that it&amp;rsquo ; s a joy to hear him effortlessly float over the disco-laden Feels So Good or put a different spin on Billy Joel&amp;rsquo ; s Just the Way You Are . In a perfect world , he would be as popular as John Legend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;15424;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;15460;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;Les Deuxluxes&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Lighter Fluid&lt;/strong&gt; **29;15496;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anna Fraces Meyer ( vocals/tenor-guitarist ) and multi-instrumentalist Etienne Barry waste no time in cranking up the noise in the garage on this excellently fun 11-song record . Nam presents a new style of K-Pop with Before We Beginhttp : **35;16356;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;16393;TOOLONG ... title= " Eric Nam plays Vogue Theatre on March 6. " alt= " Eric Nam plays Vogue Theatre on March 6. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16429;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1226636 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Eric Nam&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Before We Begin world tour&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;16459;TOOLONG : March 6 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;16494;TOOLONG : Vogue Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $45 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;16530;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;16565;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Congratulations is not standard K-Pop . The single from Eric Nam&amp;rsquo ; s Before We Begin featuring American pop singer Marc E. Bassy boasts all the meticulous hooks and slinky production fans of the genre demand . But its chorus is anything but the usual soft-core romance resonating in global hits such as BTS&amp;rsquo ; Save Me , GFriend&amp;rsquo ; s Novillera , or vintage classics such as PSY&amp;rsquo ; s Gangnam Style . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **32;16594;TOOLONG finally leaving/Let 's throw a party here tonight/And toast to the end of you and I &lt;/em&gt; is more of a kiss-off than a kiss me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before We Begin is Nam&amp;rsquo ; s first all-English album , and the Korean-American singer &amp;mdash ; who was named GQ Korea&amp;rsquo ; s 2016 man of the year and flagged by Forbes as one of the 30 Under 30 Asia 2017 &amp;mdash ; wanted to dish up something slightly different than the chart-topping , R&amp;amp ; B-tinged tunes on EPs such as Cloud 9 and Honestly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While some of the new album&amp;rsquo ; s tunes are English-language reworking of past hits , Nam wanted to offer up something different to potential new fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nam has long been made in South Korea and across much of Asia , performing at major festivals . He also is a regular presence on TV , hosting programs such as Ariang TV&amp;rsquo ; s After School Club , the ASC After Show , and even hosted a pop-up restaurant program devoted to cuisine cooked by an international group of grandmas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Postmedia chatted with the Atlanta-born and raised celebrity : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Given your Atlanta roots and Boston College education in business , how did you wind up a star in Korea ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;16628;TOOLONG : It was a combination of a lot of luck , ( and ) a lot of hard work that lead me to this . I had accepted a position as a business analyst at Deloitte Consulting in New York . But before I went into that workforce , I decided to take a year off and went to India to do a social enterprise fellowship . It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the best fit , but that was where a TV show in Korea found me and invited me to first come perform . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was it an instant fit for you being in the K-Pop scene ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;16660;TOOLONG : Not at all . Trying to establish my base took a few years . I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know the culture well , or how to fit in , and must have met with at least 15 labels before finding something that worked . Record deals there can be for singles , or they can be for a certain time period . I put in a stipulation that if my album wasn&amp;rsquo ; t out in a year , I could leave to go back to Deloitte . I signed August of 2012 , and by January 2013 I had my first album out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : It seems like many artists are successfully moving out of Korea and into the global market . And they do it without English , too . What made you decide to make the new album in English ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;16692;TOOLONG : When I first toured North America , everyone was saying that it was working because there is such as a solid Korean fan base here . The truth is , that is less than five per cent of the audience , which is mixed of all backgrounds . I think K-Pop is something that sucks people in because it&amp;rsquo ; s open . I can do pop , EDM , rock , R&amp;amp ; B and it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t matter , K-Pop embraces them all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Plus , you crank the performance aspect up a lot do n't you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;16724;TOOLONG : There was a point when dancing to music became cheesy after the boy band era of NSync and Backstreet Boys , but there were always those who craved that kind of visual satisfaction . K-Pop really filled that void , because it&amp;rsquo ; s so geared to spectacle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you think you have paved the way to build a global career from your K-Pop position ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; It hasn&amp;rsquo ; t really happened unless you are talking BTS &amp;mdash ; and they can do anything they want to because they are so big . For a solo singer/songwriter like me , it&amp;rsquo ; s new . But I have done a lot of collaborating with acts like Solange , Timbaland and others and made a point to mix English into my Korean songs . Yes , the new album was a risk , but I enjoy a challenge and pushing boundaries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;16756;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;16791;TOOLONG &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;16827;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16869;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;16905;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;16936;TOOLONG ... latest music **25;16972;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;16999;TOOLONG " **36;17030;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;17068;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;17097;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 02 Mar 2020 22:08:43 **45;17128;TOOLONG ... things to know about Miss Anthropocene by Grimeshttp : **34;17175;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17211;TOOLONG ... title= " Grimes . Promo shot for Miss Anthropocene release . 2020 PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Grimes . Promo shot for Miss Anthropocene release . 2020 PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17247;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1219584 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Miss Anthropocene&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **35;17277;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Grimes Creative Corporation/Crystal Math Music&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; In only a decade , Grimes ( a.k.a . Vancouver-born and raised Claire Boucher ) has gone from the eerie DYI of 2010&amp;rsquo ; s Halifaxa to 2012&amp;rsquo ; s confident and expansive critically acclaimed Visions right up to the wildly ambitious and successful sounds of 2015&amp;rsquo ; s Art Angels . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now Miss Anthropocene arrives at a time when Grimes is as much a global brand for her romantic affiliation with Elon Musk and shrewd manipulation of social media platforms as for her music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her fifth album is either a concept album about anthropomorphizing climate change into an evil entity , or another delightfully damming confusion of sounds and statements from an artist who is &amp;ldquo ; putting out her final earth album this year . &amp;rdquo ; This may very well be because she is fed up with all the attention being paid to her amorous adventures rather than her adventurous art . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spoiler alert : Repeated listenings won&amp;rsquo ; t divulge any grand statements about impending global chaos , save for the advice to gleefully &amp;ldquo ; f&amp;ndash ; k the world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However the 11 tracks ( or 15 on the superior deluxe edition of the album ) are philosophically assembled , Miss Anthropocene lands as one of the first big releases of this decade and certainly one of 2020&amp;rsquo ; s most-anticipated records . Here are five things to know about it : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 . So Heavy I Fell Through the Earth&lt;/strong&gt; . A booming bass line haunts the back of the mix while leaden drums pound out echoing patterns until a vocal bursts forth to lets us all know what Enya huffing helium would sound like . The layers keep on stacking onto the six-minute song , until the lightest thing about it is Grimes repeating the tune&amp;rsquo ; s title . The sudden 360 turnaround at the 4:30 mark is quite brilliant , as she apparently falls clean through the planet and its gravity to float in some ambient expanse . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 . Violence ( with io ) &lt;/strong&gt; . This uptempo electro banger is one of the most straightforward songs on the record . But with lyrics such as You wan na make me bad , make me bad/ ( And I like it like that , and I like it like that ) , it turns the coquettish pop cluttering up the radio into something far darker and disturbing . Fans will be reading into the juxtaposing of violence , pain , partying and more as some kind of commentary on her relationship . Or Grimes is just eviscerating everyone&amp;rsquo ; s expectations in public . The video is well worth a peek. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;17314;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;17350;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 . New Gods . &lt;/strong&gt; Even someone used to making sense of the words on extreme black metal recordings could have trouble translating what Grimes is actually saying . Her voice is so often mutated past the point of comprehension , or pinging around the vast echoing background as it does in this song , that you are hard-pressed to pry any kind of meaning out of the music . Which works for a project that is meant to be both ethereal and personal . The mood is as , or more , important than the meaning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 . You 'll miss me when I 'm not around&lt;/strong&gt; . From not-fully-realized drum and bass tracks ( 4&amp;AElig ; M ) to the weirdly Taylor Swift-ish acoustic strumming in Delete Forever , the album mixes somewhat experimental approaches with mainstream songcraft . Nowhere is this more realized than in the full-on dance-rock of this hooky track . The heavy guitars coupled with the blasts of mega-manipulated vocal harmonies make this a likely single . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 . We Appreciate Power&lt;/strong&gt; . Available on the deluxe version of the album , this earlier collaboration with Hana came complete with press statements that it was influenced by everything from pro-A.I. pop bands to ideas of transhumanist desire . OK computer . What is clear is that the song&amp;rsquo ; s title is an accurate reflection of the artist&amp;rsquo ; s being . It&amp;rsquo ; s the kind of hard industrial rock track you might expect from Nine Inch Nails , but with a goofball spoken-word bit that could rival Madonna&amp;rsquo ; s bad rapping in Vogue . In other words , it&amp;rsquo ; s more fun than scary . The other remixes on the deluxe edition are of varying quality , with the Algorithm mix of IDORU particularly good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Also out this week : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;17386;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;17422;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;Agnes Obel&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Myopia &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Universal Music Canada&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At some point , Obel&amp;rsquo ; s classical orientation is going to have to take shape in an opera . The voice arrangements paired against the orchestrations in Camera&amp;rsquo ; s Rolling sound ready-made to be the opening song to some noir-esque stage play as much as any album track . And that is only the beginning of this lush chamber-pop recording&amp;rsquo ; s highlights . Obel&amp;rsquo ; s music has become even more sparse and flowing with silence used to add emotional impact to every note . Check out how Can&amp;rsquo ; t Be uses a riff almost like Laurie Anderson&amp;rsquo ; s O Superman , and pairs it with a choir that sounds straight out of the Middle Ages . Gorgeous . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;17458;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;17494;TOOLONG **33;17530;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; **38;17565;TOOLONG &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Polyvinyl Record Co. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; More digital thrashing from this chiptune quartet whose use of hacked Nintendo hardware to craft tunes has produced some truly delightful results . To prove that they are more than just a bunch of tech nerds with a love of neat noises , Lorem Ipsum ( Arctic Anthem ) derives its lyrics from Cicero&amp;rsquo ; s 1st-century BC debate about the difference between pleasure and goodness . Of course , rendered through Vocaloid treatment , you&amp;rsquo ; re listening to Latin sung by a possessed child&amp;rsquo ; s toy . Still , as one example of the group&amp;rsquo ; s album with words , so that&amp;rsquo ; s pretty good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; March 4 at the Biltmore Cabaret , 2755 Prince Edward St. Tickets and info : $24.00 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;17605;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;17640;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;17669;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;17705;TOOLONG **28;17741;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; **37;17771;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Merge Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dan Snaith puts the past five years of his life down in a way he never has before on his latest release as Caribou . The opening song , Sister , is a haunting chant about making false promises and the need to break things to change them . Coming from the maths professor-cum-electronic musician , it&amp;rsquo ; s a melancholy start to the dozen new tracks he selected out of a rumoured 900 to make this album . From the warped lounge piano jazz of Sunny&amp;rsquo ; s Theme to slinky sex groover Home , this is full of surprises . Obviously , there are some dance happy tracks too . Never Come Back will have backbone&amp;rsquo ; s slipping . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;17810;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;17846;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; **29;17882;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;All Or Nothing&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Fat Cat&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This U.K. trio really sounds like some long-lost release from the 1980s heyday of Gang of Four , Au Pairs and others was suddenly discovered , remastered and released . Songs such as Initiative , No Apologies or About You could all have been hits back then or today . Singer/guitarist Rachel Aggs has a way with laying melodic leads over top of bassist/vocalist Billy Easter and drummer/vocalist Andrew Milk&amp;rsquo ; s mechanistic rhythms that just ripples with edginess . ( Steve Bosch / PNG staff photo ) PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " Steve Bosch " **28;18224;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223518 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Kratwerk in 3-D&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;18254;TOOLONG : June 20 , 7:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;18289;TOOLONG : Queen Elizabeth Theatre , 630 Hamilton &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : On sale Feb. 27 at &lt;a href= " http : **34;18325;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;18361;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; German electronic music progenitors Kraftwerk began making its distinctive brand of synthesizer-driven , robotic-voiced pop in 1970 . Ralf H&amp;uuml ; tter and Florian Schneider went on to expand their musical vision into cutting edge performances and embrace cutting-edge musical technology ahead of most musicians . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band celebrates its 50th anniversary , with a return 3-D concert tour of North America that comes to the Queen Elizabeth Theatre on June 20. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From hits such as Autobahn and Trans-Europe Express , to more ambient releases , the group &amp;mdash ; H&amp;uuml ; tter , Henning Schmitz , Fritz Hilpert and Falk Grieffenhagen &amp;mdash ; has staged 3-D performances of key albums in its catalogue at prestigious venues such as MOMA ( NYC ) , the Tate Modern Turbine Hall ( London ) and the Guggenheim Museum ( Bilbao ) . The two groups co-headline the Unity Tour this Fall with dates across North America . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 11-concert tour kicks off on Sept. 5 in Toronto , and winds down at the Hollywood Bowl on Oct. 2 . The tour comes to Pepsi Live at Rogers Arena in Vancouver on Sept. 24. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pet Shop Boys dropped their 14th album last year . Hotspot hit number-one on Billboard&amp;rsquo ; s Top Dance/Electronic Albums chart . This marked the fourth time the duo of Neil Tennant and Chris Lowe has landed an album on the top spot , which goes a long way to explaining the three-time Brit Award-winners&amp;rsquo ; $50 million in global sales since forming in 1981 . Besides the Unity Tour , the Pet Shop Boys are embarking on a greatest hits tour of Europe and the UK in May . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No word on what kind of setlist the Pet Shop Boys plan to play on this coming tour , but you just know that they will fit West End Girls into the mix . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hot off the critically acclaimed live album and documentary around their concept concerts with with designer Liam Gillick , New Order is entering the 2020s at the top of its game . The reinvigorated sound the band displayed on 2015&amp;rsquo ; s excellent Music Complete , and its companion remix release , appears to have lit up the band&amp;rsquo ; s live performances as well &amp;mdash ; with New Order touring regularly to packed houses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18879;TOOLONG ... title= " Bernard Sumner of the English rock band New Order performs at the 2013 Osheaga Music Festival at Jean-Drapeau Park in Montreal on Sunday , August 4 , 2013 . ( Dario Ayala / THE GAZETTE ) " alt= " Bernard Sumner of the English rock band New Order performs at the 2013 Osheaga Music Festival at Jean-Drapeau Park in Montreal on Sunday , August 4 , 2013 . ( Dario Ayala / THE GAZETTE ) " data-portal-copyright= " Dario Ayala " **28;18915;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223336 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band&amp;rsquo ; s status as key purveyors of post-punk dance/rock continues to grown each year . When you write a single as groundbreaking as Blue Monday , that&amp;rsquo ; s what happens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;18945;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;18981;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;19017;TOOLONG " **36;19048;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;19086;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;19115;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 24 Feb 2020 20:03:13 **45;19146;TOOLONG ... Island accepting applications for 2020-2021 busking programhttp : **33;19193;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19228;TOOLONG ... title= " Busker Keegan Chen performs at Granville Island in Vancouver , BC , February 23 , 2020. " alt= " Busker Keegan Chen performs at Granville Island in Vancouver , BC , February 23 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;19264;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223059 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; So you think you can busk ? Here&amp;rsquo ; s your chance to prove it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spring applications are now being accepted for Granville Island&amp;rsquo ; s 2020-2021 busking program and you&amp;rsquo ; ll need to know more than just the chords to Wonderwall or that Green Day song . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , if you&amp;rsquo ; re a busker performing three days or more per week , you&amp;rsquo ; ll need to have at least three hours of repertoire on hand , with no song being played more than once in a set , among other requirements . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Claire Diamond is the program coordinator for Granville Island&amp;rsquo ; s busking program and says there are about 150 applications each year , which is why specific requirements have been put in place to help whittle down the often overwhelming stack of resumes , as coordinators search for that elusive star quality to add to the Granville Island mix . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; ve got something extra special that they bring to it , &amp;rdquo ; said Diamond , of what makes a performer stand out from a sea of aspiring guitarists , entertainers , and magicians. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s having that kind of persona and kind of engagement with people and being versatile and being able to be responsive to different groups , whether that be a school group visiting or local regulars , and engaging with people beyond just the music , the personality side of it as well . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The program was created to manage the myriad of busking opportunities on Granville Island , which draws some 10.5 million visitors annually from both near and far . Applicants must fill out an application and submit a video audition ; only applicants who meet the requirements and pass an invite-only live audition each year are issued a busking license , which permits the busker to perform at designated locations throughout the island . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In previous years , acts have ranged from musicians and performers of all genres and ages &amp;ndash ; including one busker , &lt;a href= " https : **33;19294;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Eugene Dudding&lt;/a&gt; , who last year&amp;nbsp ; celebrated 30 years of busking as an acoustic guitarist . Another popular returning act over the years is &lt;a href= " https : **33;19329;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Bryon from England&lt;/a&gt; , a comedic escape show act that likes to remind audiences frequently that he is , yes , from England . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;19364;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;19400;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; While some buskers perform as a hobby or only part-time , others are full-time buskers , gigging at Granville Island in addition to other shows around the city . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the video audition portion of the application , Diamond recommended submitting a video that shows an artist performing for an audience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then that kind of gives us an idea that you do have experience performing in front of people . That&amp;rsquo ; s not obligatory but it just gives us a better image as to how you work with an audience , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Don&amp;rsquo ; t get me wrong , we still have the odd living room video . Obviously , if you&amp;rsquo ; ve got talent that&amp;rsquo ; s going to shine through irrespective of where your video is happening . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The full guidelines for busking applications is &lt;a href= " https : **33;19436;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;available online&lt;/a&gt; . The &lt;a href= " https : **33;19471;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;deadline for spring applications for the 2020-2021 program is March 8&lt;/a&gt; , while the fall round of applications are open in August and September . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there is no fee to submit an application , selected buskers will need to pay a licensing fee . A total of about 80 or so licenses will be issued . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " mailto:sip@postmedia.com " **31;19506;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;19539;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;19566;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; Stephanie IpSun , 23 Feb 2020 22:31:39 **45;19599;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicAlma Deutscher dishes on writing operas , ballroom dancing in Vienna and playing with the VSOhttp : **37;19646;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19685;TOOLONG ... title= " Child prodigy pianist/violinist and composer Alma Deutscher will perform five shows with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra . She is just 15. " alt= " Child prodigy pianist/violinist and composer Alma Deutscher will perform five shows with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra . She is just 15. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19721;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1207185 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Alma Deutscher with the VSO&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;19751;TOOLONG : Feb. 28 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;19786;TOOLONG : Bell Performing Arts Centre , 6250 144 St. , Surrey &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;19822;TOOLONG : Feb. 29 , 30 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;19857;TOOLONG : Orpheum Theatre , 601 Smithe , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;19893;TOOLONG : March 1 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;19928;TOOLONG : Centennial Theatre , 2300 Lonsdale , North Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and Info&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " https : **33;19964;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;19999;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Composer Alma Deutscher premiered her Piano Concerto in E-flat major at the Carinthian Summer Music Festival in Austria in 2017 . She also performed her Violin Concerto No. 1 in G at the same event . Only a year earlier , conductor Zubin Mehta premiered her full-length opera , Cinderella , in Vienna . That&amp;rsquo ; s quite an impressive list of achievement for any classical musician . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher was 12. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Born in Basingstoke , England , in 2005 , the musical prodigy composed her first piano sonata at age six . At seven , she had penned a short opera based upon author Neil Gaiman&amp;rsquo ; s short story the Sweeper of dreams . Three years later , she produced Cinderella . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By that time , the world had taken notice of the confident young artist&amp;rsquo ; s prodigious talents , with many in the press dubbing her &amp;ldquo ; the next Mozart . &amp;rdquo ; Unwilling to wear that title , and noting that there already was a Mozart , the artist has gone on record stating she would prefer to be recognized for who she is : Alma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vienna is an incredibly inspiring place to grow up , it is really the city of music , &amp;rdquo ; said Deutscher , over the phone from her home in Austria . &amp;ldquo ; When I go to the vineyards with the amazing view of the city to skip around in the nature , or when I&amp;rsquo ; m sitting in the underground , I&amp;rsquo ; m constantly getting ideas . Also , having lived here awhile , I&amp;rsquo ; ve also composed a few Viennese waltzes. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These waltzes will almost certainly be imbued with the light and feathery touch that characterize the era when the House of Habsburg ruled the region . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Critics writing about Deutscher&amp;rsquo ; s work have lit upon how it is very focused on melody , running counter to many of the atonal and arrhythmic facets of contemporary classical music . This somewhat tiresome argument bred in an academic circles that contemporary music must be ugly and dissonant to reflect these qualities in the world doesn&amp;rsquo ; t impress Alma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her lush , symphonic music defies the critics . They certainly can&amp;rsquo ; t accuse her of failing to find inspiration in those same mechanics and technology of everyday life . Her recently composed Siren Sounds Waltz is informed by exactly what modernists say music should embrace . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It just happens to be melodic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Viennese police siren is made in fourths&amp;nbsp ; and doesn&amp;rsquo ; t wail like most do in other places , so I decided to make it into a melody , &amp;rdquo ; Deutscher said . &amp;ldquo ; Then I also incorporated specific sounds such as local bus doors closing and other city noise and integrated them into a waltz . It begins very chaotically , with the orchestra playing like a street with horns and sirens , until it calms down and you enter into the imagination . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;20031;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;20067;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; That imaginary realm is where the &amp;ldquo ; ugly sounds of the modern world become something that is beautiful . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher&amp;rsquo ; s imaginary realm is inseparable from her musical output . She has conceived a land she calls Transylvanian &amp;mdash ; never having been to Transylvania , she says she has no idea how that name entered her head &amp;mdash ; which is where she hears beautiful melodies that wind-up in finished works . These are often written by various uniquely-named composers in the land , such as Antonin Yellowsink. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All the composers in my imaginary country write a little bit differently , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Antonin is quite a melancholy character who writes sad melodies . Sometimes , when I&amp;rsquo ; m stuck in my compositions and don&amp;rsquo ; t know what to do , I show them to him and he often makes very good suggestions . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The strong female characters in her operas undergo adventures in Transylvanian too . Deutscher has always loved stories about girls who overcame difficulty through their talents and their determination . Due to this , she populates her works with them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s what I like , and it is also is a way for me to address how , in the past , there were talented female musicians who were denied the right to compose and have their works performed , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; That really made me rather quite angry . When I write a piece , working hard to develop the melodies into a something that makes senses , with a structure that makes sense ; I think of them . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The more the interview continues , the more it becomes clear why Alma has enchanted everyone from Zubin Mehta to some of the classical music world&amp;rsquo ; s toughest critics and beloved British presenter Stephen Fry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her love of music is absolute , and completely impossible to separate from any other facet of her life . This artist was finding ways to note down the melodies that apparently come into her head constantly when she was six years-old and had no formal musical training to speak of . Her ongoing studying has , in large part , been about developing better techniques to transition what she hears to sheet music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deutscher went on the record at age 10 stating that she understood how it might be hard for some to take her music seriously because of her years . The 15 year-old on the other end of the line exudes a professional confidence coupled with youthful energy that begs the question : How far can it go ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m always composing , but it&amp;rsquo ; s a very hard and time-consuming process , taking those melodies that are constantly coming and organizing them , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; And for pieces such as the piano concerto I&amp;rsquo ; m doing in Vancouver , which I wrote when I was 12 , I am always doing little fixes and changes . I re-orchestrated almost the whole concerti to put in a big brass section , because I&amp;rsquo ; ve always wanted it to have a big crashing section that&amp;rsquo ; s really loud , and never felt that it worked quite right . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She says that the piano concerto holds a particular place in her heart , because she wrote a good deal of it on her grandmother&amp;rsquo ; s piano after she passed on . She says that it&amp;rsquo ; s one of the saddest things she has written , surprising to people who know what an incredibly happy person she is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So what does she do besides music ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love ballroom dancing , and Vienna is a perfect place for that , and it&amp;rsquo ; s become a serious passion , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Twice a week , I get to go and learn the most amazing dances , and I&amp;rsquo ; m going to go to lots of balls this coming season with my dresses already picked out . And I also love the theatre . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alma Deutscher will play three performances with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra under composer Stanley Dodds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " sderdeyn@postmedia.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;20103;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;20141;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;20170;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 21 Feb 2020 19:00:30 **45;20201;TOOLONG ... announcement : The Weeknd opens new world tour in Vancouverhttp : **31;20248;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;20281;TOOLONG ... title= " The Weeknd play at the end of the H&amp;amp ; M STUDIO SS17 Spring-Summer 2017 ready-to-wear collection fashion show on March 1 , 2017 in Paris . " alt= " The Weeknd play at the end of the H&amp;amp ; M STUDIO SS17 Spring-Summer 2017 ready-to-wear collection fashion show on March 1 , 2017 in Paris . " data-portal-copyright= " GEOFFROY VAN DER HASSELT " **28;20317;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220557 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Weeknd : The After Hours Tour&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;20347;TOOLONG : June 11 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;20382;TOOLONG : Pepsi Live at Rogers Arena , 800 Griffiths Way &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : Feb. 25 , 10 a.m. at &lt;a href= " https : **33;20418;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;20453;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Weeknd will open his new world tour in Vancouver on June 11 at Rogers Arena . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The After Hours Tour takes its name from the Toronto hitmaker&amp;rsquo ; s upcoming album , After Hours , which drops on March 20 . The 57-date tour includes dates in Canada , the US , UK and Europe . Sabrina Claudio and Don Toliver will be the support for the U.S. dates with 88Glam and Sabrina Claudio being the special guests for European dates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In keeping with his taste for elaborate stage designs , the Weeknd says the tour will showcase a stage with the most LED lights and video for an arena show . The artist , born Abel Makkonen Tesfaye , last toured in 2017 . The Starboy : Legend of the Fall show was notable for its lively and shimmering light show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Hours is the fourth full-length album from the artist , who has elevated his alternative R&amp;amp ; B sound to the top of the charts . The single Blinding Lights from the upcoming release became the Weeknd&amp;rsquo ; s fourth number one single on the Billboard Hot 100 Chart . The title track was released on Feb. 18. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For all North American dates , each ticket purchased online comes with a copy of the new album . &amp;nbsp ; The Weeknd&amp;rsquo ; s next performance will be Saturday Night Live on March 7. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;20485;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;20521;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;20557;TOOLONG " **36;20588;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;20626;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;20655;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynThu , 20 Feb 2020 18:53:59 **45;20686;TOOLONG ... Baker keeps dad Ginger 's beat alive with The Music of Creamhttp : **35;20733;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;20770;TOOLONG ... title= " Kofi Baker , drummer , son of the late Ginger Baker and one of the forces behind The Music of Cream . " alt= " Kofi Baker , drummer , son of the late Ginger Baker and one of the forces behind The Music of Cream . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;20806;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1219583 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Music Of Cream : 50th Anniversary World Tour featuring Disraeli Gears&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;20836;TOOLONG : Sat . Feb. 29 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;20871;TOOLONG : Rickshaw Theatre , 254 E. Hastings St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $25 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;20907;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;20942;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; For a band that was together only slightly more than two years ( 1966 to 1968 ) , Cream certainly left a legacy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The trio of drummer Ginger Baker , bassist/vocalist Jack Bruce and guitarist/vocalist Eric Clapton were the ultimate late-&amp;rsquo ; 60s power trio , responsible for taking the blues rock of the day into amplified zones of proto-heavy metal and jam band excess . Along the way , the group became the first supergroup to come out of the British Invasion . Each of the three musicians had served time in other popular bands of the day such as the Graham Bond Organization ( Baker , Bruce ) , the Yardbirds ( Clapton ) and John Mayall&amp;rsquo ; s Bluesbreakers ( Clapton , Bruce ) before coming together to form the far more popular trio . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over the course of four studio albums and one live disc , the band produced such classic rock radio fixtures as Sunshine of Your Love , I Feel Free and Badge . Clapton went on to become an AOR legend . Baker and Bruce never attained near the same level of success again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , any music fan out there should check out Baker&amp;rsquo ; s excellent Air Force albums as well as 1990&amp;rsquo ; s Middle Passage . Ditto for Bruce&amp;rsquo ; s post-Cream output such as 1993s Somethin&amp;rsquo ; Else or his work with NYC underground composer Kip Hanrahan ( 1985&amp;rsquo ; s Vertical&amp;rsquo ; s Currency ; 1988&amp;rsquo ; s Days and Nights of Blue Luck Inverted ) . Bruce died in 2014 at age 71 , and Baker died last October at age 80. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Keeping the late player&amp;rsquo ; s music alive is the reason behind &lt;a href= " https : //www.musicofcream.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Music of Cream&lt;/a&gt; . Not only does the band include Eric Clapton&amp;rsquo ; s nephew , Will Johns &amp;mdash ; the son of Led Zeppelin and Rolling Stones sound engineer Andy Johns &amp;mdash ; on guitar and vocals , but its beat is held down by Kofi Baker , Ginger&amp;rsquo ; s son . Musicians Sean McNabb and Chris Shutters round out the group , proving that it takes more than three musicians to recreate that distinctive Cream sound . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The group is touring in memoriam to Baker , who died Oct. 6 , playing Cream&amp;rsquo ; s landmark 1967 second album , Disraeli Gears , in its entirety . Considered by many critics to be the best thing the band ever recorded , the album includes songs such as Sunshine of Your Love , Strange Brew , Tales of Brave Ulysses and more . The Music of Cream performs the entire album and rounds out the set with a selection of other material , including &amp;ldquo ; Clapton Classics&amp;rdquo ; such as Cocaine , Layla and Wonderful Tonight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kofi Baker chatted to Postmedia prior to the tour starting from his home in Chicago . The drummer , who has worked with such luminaries as jazz bassist Jonas Hellborg , started The Music of Cream following the original band&amp;rsquo ; s 2005 reunion show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Postmedia : Obviously , you have a direct family connection to the music of Cream . But what made you decide that this project needed to happen at the time that you launched it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Kofi Baker&lt;/strong&gt; : I went to the Madison Square Garden show in 2006 when the band reformed and , at that time , I was doing mainly fusion jazz and hadn&amp;rsquo ; t really considered playing Cream music . But after the gig , my dad was saying he&amp;rsquo ; d never play with Jack again , had all the money he needed and that was that . So I decided to play it because people had always asked me to , it looked like a lot of fun and there was plenty of opportunity for improvisation and what I liked in music . Obviously , after my dad passed on , it took on a different feel as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Your press suggests that this project has experienced something of a similar up and down ride as that of the original band did ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;20971;TOOLONG : Oh yeah , every time things started to take hold , someone else came along with an idea that wrecked it and destroyed it and we had to regroup once again . One of the worst suggestions was having Malcolm ( Bruce , Jack&amp;rsquo ; s son ) join the band and being billed as the &amp;ldquo ; Sons of Cream . &amp;rdquo ; Unfortunately , Malcolm doesn&amp;rsquo ; t know any Cream songs , doesn&amp;rsquo ; t like playing them and he&amp;rsquo ; s not really a bass player . So that never worked and now he is out and we have much better musicians , a real bass player and it&amp;rsquo ; s a much better project . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21003;TOOLONG ... title= " The band Cream in 1968 , its psychedelic heyday : Drummer Ginger Baker , guitarist Eric Clapton and bassist Jack Bruce ( left to right ) . " alt= " The band Cream in 1968 , its psychedelic heyday : Drummer Ginger Baker , guitarist Eric Clapton and bassist Jack Bruce ( left to right ) . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout/Postmedia News files " **28;21039;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1220038 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : So it takes four of you to do what the three of them did ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;21069;TOOLONG : Not at all , we just like to have the option of the larger band for the Clapton classics such as Layla and to flesh out the songs by Blind Faith and so on . Even Eric never plays Cream songs as a trio anymore , as he says he likes the larger sound . I&amp;rsquo ; m more of a full drummer than my dad , coming from the busier fusion side of things , so I tend to the more high-energy attack that Cream played with in the &amp;rsquo ; 60s than that super clean , stripped-down style that they brought to the material in the reunion . It was great , but it was kind of laid back . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You 've played professionally since you were 17 , starting off with Steve Marriott ( Small Faces , Humble Pie ) and in groups with everyone from the late guitar genius Shawn Lane to Megadeth spin-offs . What did your father think of your work ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;21101;TOOLONG : Well , obviously he was there at the start teaching me things . But the last 10 years of his life , a man who was never that easy to deal with , had become someone we thought was so much worse . We were completely estranged . But when I went to see him in hospital , I came to realize that it was his wife who was really the reason that he wasn&amp;rsquo ; t communicating with the rest of the family , making him write us out of his will and so on . None of us was even invited to the funeral until the last minute , so most of us couldn&amp;rsquo ; t make it . But I knew that was all because of her , which makes it better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : All the more reason to have this project take shape and keep the family legacy going . Are there any songs that you find really stand out for you ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;21133;TOOLONG : It&amp;rsquo ; s interesting , because I like pretty much all of it . White Room and Tales are kind of the same song , and Badge is pretty straight-ahead pop . But the rest is unique , falling somewhere in between jam bands , heavy metal and even aspects of fusion . To my hearing , no one seems to have done that since with both that level of musicianship as well as keeping it heavy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;21165;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;21200;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;21236;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;21267;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;21305;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;21334;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;21365;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;21407;TOOLONG ... Vancouver Opera 's The Barber of Seville fails to catch firehttp : **36;21736;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;21774;TOOLONG ... title= " The sets are pure comic book whimsy for Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . " alt= " The sets are pure comic book whimsy for Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . " data-portal-copyright= " David Cooper " **28;21810;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206415 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Barber of Seville&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Continues &lt;strong&gt; Thursday , Feb. 20&lt;/strong&gt; at 7:30 p.m. and &lt;strong&gt;Sunday , Feb. 23&lt;/strong&gt; at 2 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Queen Elizabeth Theatre , &lt;/strong&gt; 630 Hamilton St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets , information : &lt;/strong&gt; From $41.75 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;21840;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;21875;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver Opera&amp;rsquo ; s latest production of Rossini&amp;rsquo ; s perennial favourite The Barber of Seville has a lot going for it . From the moment the curtain rises &amp;mdash ; after , for once , an overture played as an overture with no superfluous stage action &amp;mdash ; we are presented with an environment that signals &amp;ldquo ; Comedy Tonight ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ken MacDonald&amp;rsquo ; s set is gloriously daffy but sophisticated . Director Ashlie Corcoran , whose day job is artistic director of Arts Club Theatre , creates a staging filled with coy , comic turns . Her approach is inventive and loaded with details , both broad and fine , that should have served the production well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Barber&amp;rsquo ; s big numbers are democratically handed out to a generally strong ensemble cast . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Count Almaviva , Victoria tenor Isaiah Bell was uneven . Mezzo Julie Boulianne as Rosina was spot on , with a rich tone , good flexibility and a strong sense of Rossini style . Baritone Taehyun Jun was delightful in the role of Don Basilio , vocally first rate and entirely prepared to enter into the comic spirit of his part and the production . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lyric baritone Edward Nelson made a very fine Figaro indeed , a congenital schemer who&amp;rsquo ; s always a step , or three , ahead of everyone else . Confident yet not bombastic , Nelson makes it clear that Figaro is well aware that clever counts for only so much in a world of privilege and power . And he was able to sail through all the nimble moves demanded by Corcoran with ease . He was a joy to watch every second he was on the stage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21908;TOOLONG ... title= " California baritone Edward Nelson sings Figaro in Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . " alt= " California baritone Edward Nelson sings Figaro in Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . " data-portal-copyright= " Arielle Doneson " **28;21944;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206414 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; So with all that is good about the production , why did I leave the theatre disappointed that one of my favourite comic operas failed to catch fire ? It&amp;rsquo ; s all about timing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canadian conductor Nathan Brock , back in this country after stints in Europe , flattered his singers and rarely pushed his orchestra out of its comfort zones . This wasn&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily a problem in arias and ensemble pieces , but in the copious recitatives that drive the story , an overriding desire for vocal beauty was at odds with pacing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Comfort also killed the comedy . The first act dragged , while the second act never quite got off the ground . There was a surfeit of caution mixed with self-conscious cuteness . ( Do we really need a whiff of Wagner&amp;rsquo ; s Treulich gef&amp;uuml ; hrt in the fortepiano to underscore an impending marriage ? Oh , so precious ; oh , so opera insider. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What could &amp;mdash ; I&amp;rsquo ; d argue should &amp;mdash ; have been played as breakneck farce , languished . And with all this loving lingering , one had time to see how calculated and thin a goodly number of Corcoran&amp;rsquo ; s cunning ideas proved . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the opening night audience was amused , Rossini would have preferred them rolling in the aisles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;21974;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;22016;TOOLONG ... music preview : Vancouver Opera gives Barber of Seville a new stylish cut&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;22051;TOOLONG ... latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;22086;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;22113;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsMusicDJ-producer Jacques Greene develops new styles on Dawn Chorushttp : **34;22323;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;22359;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal musician and DJ Jacques Greene performs at The Beaumont in Vancouver on Feb. 28. " alt= " Montreal musician and DJ Jacques Greene performs at The Beaumont in Vancouver on Feb. 28. " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;22395;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211673 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Jacques Greene Live Set&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;22425;TOOLONG : Friday , Feb. 28 , 10 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;22460;TOOLONG : The Beaumont Studios , 316 West 5th Ave. , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $20 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;22496;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;22531;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jacques Greene makes stylish music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His brand of trippy house beats come ready-made to move underground tastemakers , movers and shakers . So much so that it&amp;rsquo ; s no surprise to know the Toronto-based producer has inked deals with two fashion designers for his Vase Records label . You need to look as good as you sound , right ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Born Philippe Aubin-Dionne in Montreal , Greene came to national and international recognition with his 2017 debut Feel Infinite . A Polaris Prize longer list album nominee , the recording arrived nearly seven years after the producer first made waves with a single on the 2010 Night Slugs Allstars compilation. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A mix of minimalism , melody , R&amp;amp ; B snippets and assorted house and techno tricks , the recording boasted highlights such as Afterglow , True ( feat . How to Dress Well ) and the title track . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Greene was clearly an artist who could expand the sonic range of dance music to include emotion and introspection more commonly sourced in soul and urban pop . But he never lost the train of thought that says keep the party bumping . His latest release , Dawn Chorus , is an equally engaging and emotional collection loaded with guests and collaborators ranging from Lost in Translation film composer Brian Reitzell to rapper Cadence Weapon and ambient vocalist Julianna Barwick. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a big leap in arrangements , production and pulse for the artist , who has moved from expertise in cutting and pasting collages of samples to working from a live musician base to reconfigure into something new . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Green took time to chat from his Toronto studio prior to heading across Europe and North America on tour , noting that new material such as Serenity was &amp;ldquo ; pulling off the magic trick&amp;rdquo ; of keeping audiences entranced. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;22559;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;22595;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : I 've read about five different reviews of Dawn Chorus , and each talks about how you have " updated " house music . Why is that , and has house become the heavy metal of electronic music , that just never goes away ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;22631;TOOLONG : At this point house music can be more of a catch-all word for any and all dance music that isn&amp;rsquo ; t grim or very minimal and cold sounding . Along with acid house , which is still around as well , it&amp;rsquo ; s a type of updated disco and it keeps coming back around . When done right , it&amp;rsquo ; s grooves in pockets with nice melodies that are fun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : It 's also the electronic music genre that lends itself best to the formation of actual songs rather than just tracks , is n't it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;22663;TOOLONG : Yep , the most exciting house music is when it hovers between song and track in its complexity . A classic Chez Damier or Little Louie gives you a little more than just a bass line and a rimshot pattern . My ear is drawn to things with a few more of those elements of melody and hints of structure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Besides composing your own original material , you have a lengthy resum ? remixing artists ranging from Radiohead to Ciara and others . What makes a great remix in your mind ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;22695;TOOLONG : I think you need to study the parts of the original as hard as you can and focus in on one section that you really hook on , like the way the vocal sounds in the pre-chorus break , or something . Not that it has to be one eight-second loop , but more that one section that you can extend and expand upon until it really catches your ear and almost trying to drive someone insane with it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Dawn Chorus is a different thing from what you just described , involving a lot more input from musicians and singers . Do you think that recording at Hudson Mohawke 's L.A. studio got under your skin and inspired some of these changes , bringing kind of an L.A. vibe ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;22727;TOOLONG : Yeah , that place is infectious and gets right under your skin . Part of the genesis of the album was , how could I use the tools &amp;mdash ; drum machines and such &amp;mdash ; that belong to the culture of the genre , but make it so if you were blindfolded and listened , you might file it under alternative pop rather than dance music . I wrote the album in my 29th year ; now I&amp;rsquo ; m 30 and not a young gun anymore , but still working in a hedonistic , youth-obsessed ecosystem . What music would make me comfortable where I&amp;rsquo ; m at now ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : A very sizable demographic that came of age in the ' 90s with the explosion of electronic music and raves is in that same place you describe now . Unlike classic rock , where you can play your hits from a walker , electronic music has never been about staying in the same place . Do you think you are paving the way for where the music can be for changing tastes ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;22759;TOOLONG : There are definitely veterans that are resting on their laurels in dance music too , but that&amp;rsquo ; s not where I&amp;rsquo ; m at . Since 2014 , I&amp;rsquo ; ve been really invested in using a lot of visuals in my sets , but trying to avoid the massive projections and LED lights that can be somewhat distracting and take you out of the experience . So I&amp;rsquo ; ve been developing my lighting set with a designer from Glasgow named Shaun Murphy to take almost standard club lighting and turn it into something new that keeps the black space there too . At least I hope so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;22791;TOOLONG " **36;22822;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;22860;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;22889;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;22920;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? " alt= " Steve Earle and The Dukes play the Burnaby Blues and Roots fest on Aug. 7. NewsMusicClassical music : Maria Callas hologram to perform with Vancouver Symphony Orchestrahttp : **31;23576;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;23609;TOOLONG ... title= " The one , the only Maria Callas. " alt= " The one , the only Maria Callas. " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;23645;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1215842 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Callas In Concert -- The Hologram Tour&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 22 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Orpheum Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Info and tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; From $40 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;23675;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;23710;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nostalgia meets state-of-the-art 21st century technology in a surprising new initiative at the Vancouver Symphony : a program focused on that most 20th century of divas , Maria Callas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is not just a tribute concert , nor a program of great hits from her characteristic repertoire . This is a technological evocation of Callas in holographic form , a ghost in the machine , mounted by production company BASE Hologram. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23742;TOOLONG ... title= " Maria Callas in 1968 " alt= " Maria Callas in 1968 " data-portal-copyright= " ALFREDO MICCOLI " **28;23778;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1218452 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hologram shows devoted to dead performing artists have been around for a while . Events built around Frank Zappa , Buddy Holly , and Roy Orbison have done well at the box office , despite some audience pushback over the ethics involved . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But it&amp;rsquo ; s a startling idea to build a show around an operatic diva . And what greater opera legend is there than Maria Callas ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Born Maria Anna Cecilia Sofia Kalogeropoulos in Manhattan in 1923 , Callas was educated in Athens , then began singing professionally in 1941 . At first she sang almost everything , even Wagner . But , under the watchful eye of Italian industrialist ( and Callas&amp;rsquo ; first husband ) Giovanni Battista Meneghini , and mentored by conductor Tullio Serafin , she reinvented herself as a bel canto specialist . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She was always controversial , always in the news . But she was remarkable . In her prime , her acting style was as inimitable as her voice . The late Alan Aberbach , founder of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Opera Club , told me about seeing all of her Met performances as Tosca one season . Each was memorable , but on one particular evening the stars aligned and Aberbach witnessed the opera performance of a lifetime as Callas took extraordinary risks and triumphed utterly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23808;TOOLONG ... title= " Maria Callas performing in Vincenzo Bellini 's Norma in Paris in 1964. " alt= " Maria Callas performing in Vincenzo Bellini 's Norma in Paris in 1964. " data-portal-copyright= " - " **28;23844;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1218456 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her private life &amp;mdash ; not that someone as newsworthy as Callas could ever be private &amp;mdash ; could be the grist for a fine opera libretto : she left Meneghini to become the mistress of Aristotle Onassis from 1959 until 1968. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then Onassis married Jacqueline Kennedy . A humiliated Callas tried film acting , then teaching ; her sessions at Juilliard became the subject of Terrence McNally&amp;rsquo ; s play Master Class . There was a profitable comeback tour ( including a March 1974 date at the Queen Elizabeth Theatre ) , but the glory days were over . She died in Paris in 1977 , aged just 53. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Talking with the VSO&amp;rsquo ; s Neil Middleton , I got a tangible sense of the orchestra bravely attempting something far outside the usual concert model . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; BASE Hologram developed the Callas show to get into the classical world with an iconic artist , and approached the orchestra . The logistics are sophisticated , explains Middleton . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit magical : proprietary technology from the company uses multiple projectors to create a 3-D image of Callas standing on the stage . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recordings of the singer are used ; her vocal part is extracted and played back while the orchestra performs live , similar to the VSO strategy for its wildly successful movie nights . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23874;TOOLONG ... title= " Irish conductor and composer E ? mear Noone . " alt= " Irish conductor and composer E ? mear Noone . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;23910;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1215841 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This demands a conductor perfectly comfortable not just with tailoring an orchestral accompaniment , but also a specialist not spooked by technology . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Middleton believes the project has just the right fit with Irish conductor and composer E&amp;iacute ; mear Noone , who crafted soundtracks for video games like Overwatch and World Of Warcraft , and recently received accolades for being the first woman to conduct the orchestra at the Oscar&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the VSO is chuffed to debut the Canadian presentation of a classical holographic project , trying out new technology is a complicated endeavour with more than a few risks involved . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To assure everyone in the audience gets the best possible experience , some Orpheum seats will be off limits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Seats have to be limited because the hologram is not perfectly visible from every point in the theatre , &amp;rdquo ; says Middleton . &amp;ldquo ; This is definitely a suspense of disbelief proposition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a chance for my generation to discover an rare talent in the only way still possible &amp;mdash ; to experience what made Callas such an iconic figure . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;23940;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;23982;TOOLONG ... about the VSO&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;24018;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;24054;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;24082;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;24118;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;24149;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? We 'd like to hear from you about this or any other stories you think we should know about .
