" data-portal-copyright= " Eduardo Verdugo " **28;24374;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1218181 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Nick Cave and the Bad Seeds Ghosteen Tour&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;24404;TOOLONG : Oct. 17 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;24439;TOOLONG : Pacific Coliseum , 100 N. Renfrew St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : On sale Feb. 21 , 10 a.m. at &lt;a href= " https : //www.ticketleader.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;24475;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Australia&amp;rsquo ; s Nick Cave and the Bad Seeds&amp;rsquo ; intense and raging brand of rock music has been blowing audiences away and winning fans for 37 years . The group , fronted by the former lead vocalist of the Birthday Party , has released seventeen albums to date , with its last three being some of the best reviewed of the group&amp;rsquo ; s entire career . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ghosteen , released in 2019 , completed a trilogy that began with 2013&amp;rsquo ; s passionate Push the Sky Away and 2016&amp;rsquo ; s soul-bearing Skeleton Key . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All three releases are imbued with pain and tragedy . In 2016 , Cave lost his son Arthur after he fell from the Ovingdean Gap near Brighton , England . In 2017 , longtime Bad Seeds keyboardist Conway Savage died from a brain tumour . &amp;nbsp ; The double-album Ghosteen is dedicated to Savage and showcases some of the most spiritual lyrics of the literate poet/author&amp;rsquo ; s career . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Approaching writing the new album , Cave is on record stating he&amp;rsquo ; &amp;ldquo ; found an authentic way to write beyond the trauma . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is also the most ambient and electronic album from a group known for crafting crashing textures with non-digital instruments . The 11-track release drew rave reviews across almost all media , winding up on most rock critics&amp;rsquo ; best of 2019 lists . Cave&amp;rsquo ; s most recent appearance in B.C. was at the Massey Theatre in New Westminster on October 10 , for one of the Conversations With Nick Cave : An Evening of Talk &amp;amp ; Music tour dates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nick Cave and the Bad Seeds tickets go on sale Feb. 21 , 10 a.m. at &lt;a href= " https : //www.ticketleader.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;24506;TOOLONG . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;24537;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;24573;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;24609;TOOLONG " **36;24640;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;24678;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;24707;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynTue , 18 Feb 2020 20:35:38 **45;24738;TOOLONG ... things to know about Changes by Justin Bieberhttp : **34;24785;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;24821;TOOLONG ... title= " Singer Justin Bieber and his wife Hailey Baldwin pose at the premiere for the documentary television series " Justin Bieber : Seasons " in Los Angeles , California , U.S. , January 27 , 2020. " alt= " Singer Justin Bieber and his wife Hailey Baldwin pose at the premiere for the documentary television series " Justin Bieber : Seasons " in Los Angeles , California , U.S. , January 27 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " MARIO ANZUONI " **28;24857;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217112 " /&gt; **28;24887;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Justin Bieber &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;RBMG/Def Jam Recordings/Universal Music Canada&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Justin Bieber is back . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to believe that it&amp;rsquo ; s been five years since Purpose was released . Tracks such as What do You Mean ? , Company , Sorry and Love Yourself are still in such heavy rotation on radio and digital formats that it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to go a week without hearing one of those hits somewhere . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , yes , album number five is here , and it&amp;rsquo ; s one of the new decade&amp;rsquo ; s most-anticipated releases . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It arrives when the singer&amp;rsquo ; s YouTube Originals docuseries , Justin Bieber : Season , is the No. 1 artist channel on the viewing platform . His Spotify account surpasses 62 million monthly listeners , making him the streaming service&amp;rsquo ; s&amp;nbsp ; No. 1 worldwide artist . And his Changes tour kicks off on May 14 with special guests Kehlani and Jaden Smith . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets are on sale now at &lt;a href= " https : **28;24917;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;24947;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The album&amp;rsquo ; s two leadoff singles &amp;mdash ; Intentions ( feat . Quavo ) and Yummy &amp;mdash ; were both global smashes as well . At 25 , Bieber is no longer the breakout child star or the tempestuous teen troublemaker . He is an adult , married and moving into the next phase of a career that has probably already paid out enough to keep him in new hair dye jobs and tattoos for life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But how has the maturity made the music ? Here are five things to know about Changes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : All Around Me&lt;/strong&gt; . Never thought I could ever by loyal/To someone other then myself/Never thought I could ever be a spoiler/Guess anything is possible since you made my heart melt/Gave me the best hand that I&amp;rsquo ; ve ever been dealt . Yes , he&amp;rsquo ; s in love and he&amp;rsquo ; s proclaiming it from the opening song on through the other 16 tracks and the Summer Walker remix of Yummy that closes the record out . Instead of the moody self-doubt of Purpose , he is coming across bright eyed and doting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Trap-heavy R&amp;B&lt;/strong&gt; . The album is loaded with slinky fluid sonics married to sparse trap beats and Bieber&amp;rsquo ; s manipulated falsetto moaning over top ( Come Around Me , Available , Running Over , Yummy ) . The effect of having all the material coming at you in pretty much the same pace and production package is that a lot of the songs sound the same . That was doubtfully the idea behind the track sequencing . But it really just makes the better songs leap out &amp;mdash ; Forever with Post Malone and Clever is a true standout &amp;mdash ; and much of the rest of the record sound like filler . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Take It Out On Me&lt;/strong&gt; . I&amp;rsquo ; ll be your punching bag/Hit me with all of your might . Much has been written in celeb pages about how Bieber has changed and become more sensitive and spiritual . This song&amp;rsquo ; s confessional lyric plays to that argument with some delightfully crunchy keyboard backing and blank pauses . But he still manages to mention that he is his lover&amp;rsquo ; s psychiatrist as well as their miracle . Humble as ever , it appears . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;24980;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;25016;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : Second Emotion&lt;/strong&gt; . Probably the best song on the album for phrasing , this funkier number features Travis Scott mumbling for a moment . It&amp;rsquo ; s a veritable combat between the two vocalists to determine who can get the most wicked mechanized vocal treatments in the track . Scott wins , as at one point , he sounds like an drunken robot trying tripping over its digital tongue trying to finish a sentence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : E.T.A&lt;/strong&gt; . &lt;strong&gt;and Changes&lt;/strong&gt; . Every JB album needs one smooth ballad , and these two are pretty much it for Changes . With a very jazzy guitar part and almost invisible bass beat , E.T.A . is the slow dance number that fans will be flocking to . It&amp;rsquo ; s going to be a showstopper live too , no doubt . Great line : Drop a pint to me now/So I can know your location . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Also out this week : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;25052;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;25088;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Abel **25;25124;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Greetings From Stanley Park EP&lt;/em&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;25151;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;25186;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This local band fronted by singer Thorsten Abel delivers four tracks of fuzzy , psychedelic pop that falls nicely somewhere in between the Velvet Underground and Dream Syndicate with a solid serving of Brit Pop arena punch tossed in . Bores of Asstown is one of the better song titles around , but the album&amp;rsquo ; s real clencher is the lengthy Made to Measure Mae West . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Abel Collective EEP release party , Feb. 28 , 9 p.m . Hastings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;25219;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;25255;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Puss N Boots&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **35;25291;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Universal Music Canada&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Norah Jones , Sasha Dobson and Catherine Popper are Puss N Boots and the trio sounds like it&amp;rsquo ; s having a lot of fun on this 14 track record . The high lonesome harmonizing is particularly good on Popper&amp;rsquo ; s Lucky . The covers range from a so-so cover of Concrete Blonde&amp;rsquo ; s Joey to an excellent version of Tom Petty&amp;rsquo ; s Angel Dream . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;25328;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;25364;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tame Impala&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Slow Rush&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Universal Music Canada&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kevin Parker&amp;rsquo ; s psychedelic project has become one of the most influential groups in the exploding Australian music scene and its fourth album will only continue to cement that reputation . Talk : $790,000 raised for Vancouver Symphony Orchestra and VSO School of Musichttp : **35;25754;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;25791;TOOLONG ... title= " Alexandra Mauler-Steinmann and Barbara Klebanoff co-chaired the 30th-annual Vancouver Symphony Ball that benefited the VSO and its 17-year-old school of music that is unique for Canadian orchestras . " alt= " Alexandra Mauler-Steinmann and Barbara Klebanoff co-chaired the 30th-annual Vancouver Symphony Ball that benefited the VSO and its 17-year-old school of music that is unique for Canadian orchestras . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry/ " **28;25827;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214192 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; ORCHESTRAL VARIATIONS : Third-time co-chair Alexandra Mauler-Steinmann and newcomer Barbara Klebanoff fronted the 30th annual Vancouver Symphony Ball that reportedly raised $790,000 and change recently . Good news for recently appointed VSO board chair Etienne Bruson . Ditto for Angela Elster who was three weeks into her job as president-CEO of the orchestra and its unique-in-Canada music school . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25857;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra maestro Otto Tausk and new president-CEO Angela Elster made their debut appearances at the Symphony Ball . " alt= " Vancouver Symphony Orchestra maestro Otto Tausk and new president-CEO Angela Elster made their debut appearances at the Symphony Ball . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry/ " **28;25893;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214195 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As usual , the gala offered something no other can : Maestro Otto Tausk conducting the VSO through Bizet&amp;rsquo ; s Carmen Suite No. 1 and de Falla&amp;rsquo ; s Siete canciones populares espa&amp;ntilde ; olas . William Rowson then took the baton for the event&amp;rsquo ; s ever-favourite Lehar and Strauss waltzes along with foxtrots , rumbas , tangos and swing-era tunes . The gala was themed to complement a concert by the orchestra and Canadian mezzo-soprano Rihab Chaieb titled Carmen : Sounds of Spain . That induced many attendees to wear red , black and lace while tucking into Spanish appetizers and less-Hispanic ( and hardly kosher ) ham-wrapped ling cod . Two chaps sported kilts , while **26;25923;TOOLONG Rail chair David McLean wore the tartan pants that Scots call trews . Such garb intrigued Utrecht-born Tausk . Perhaps he&amp;rsquo ; ll adopt it for an Orpheum theatre workout with Mendelssohn&amp;rsquo ; s Hebrides Overture. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25951;TOOLONG ... title= " Crazy 8s directors ( front ) Brodi-Jo Scalise , Josh Aries , Daniel Irving and ( rear ) Ali Liebert , Bruna Arbex and Malibu Taetz received cash to shoot their movies in one week . " alt= " Crazy 8s directors ( front ) Brodi-Jo Scalise , Josh Aries , Daniel Irving and ( rear ) Ali Liebert , Bruna Arbex and Malibu Taetz received cash to shoot their movies in one week . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry/ " **28;25987;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214193 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; MORE CRAZINESS : &amp;ldquo ; Here&amp;rsquo ; s $800 . Go make a movie . Bring it back completed next week . &amp;rdquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s what Crazy8s organizers have told the tournament&amp;rsquo ; s finalist film directors every year since 1991 . With the cash and considerably more in donated equipment and production services , not to mention the advantage of some early-in-the-alphabet surnames , Andy Alvarez , Bruna Arbex , Josh Aries , Daniel Irving , Ali Liebert , Brodi-Jo Scalise and Malibu Taetz fanned out recently and will screen their short features at The Centre on Feb. 22 . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26561;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211247 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Doric String Quartet with Marc-Andr ? Hamelin&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; 3 p.m. , Feb. 16 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancouver Playhouse &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //vanrecital.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;26591;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Schumann Quartet&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; 3 p.m. , March 1 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancouver Playhouse &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //vanrecital.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;26621;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Pavel Haas Quartet with Boris Giltburg , piano&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; 3 p.m. , March 8 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancouver Playhouse &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **26;26651;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;26679;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite the off-again , on-again winter , February and March are prime time for two of our major chamber music presenters , who hope to lure us into the concert hall with some exceptional programs featuring the Doric , Schumann , and Pavel Hass string quartets. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; String quartets have the most exceptional repertoire depth of any chamber music configuration , and they are the core of what Friends of Chamber Music brings to town . The FOC has planned an ambitious end to its 72nd season : the Tak&amp;aacute ; cs Quartet playing all six Bart&amp;oacute ; k quartets over two evenings , April 26 and 28. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Pavel Hass Quartet will be heard March 8 in a program featuring quartets by Cekovsk&amp;aacute ; and Tchaikovsky , plus a quintet including pianist Boris Giltberg . Giltberg made his Vancouver debut in 2011 , and you can be sure that his passionate playing will be welcome in Dvor&amp;aacute ; k&amp;rsquo ; s 1887 Piano Quintet. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And there&amp;rsquo ; s some new music as well with a performance &amp;mdash ; surely a Vancouver first &amp;mdash ; of A Midsummer Quartet ( 2016 ) by Lubica Cekovsk&amp;aacute ; . Born in 1975 , the Slovakian composer studied at London&amp;rsquo ; s Royal Academy of Music and created quite a stir with her operatic treatment of The Picture of Dorian Gray . She also did the music for the 2016 computer game The Mandate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vancouver Recital Society hosts string quartets less frequently , but when founder Leila Getz finds a group she admires , you can be sure that Vancouver audiences will hear them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Doric Quartet returns to town on Feb. 16 to play quartets by Dvorak and Sibelius , plus a new composition &amp;mdash ; the Canadian premiere of Mark-Andr&amp;eacute ; Hamelin&amp;rsquo ; s Piano Quintet , with the composer at the keyboard . Hamelin is well known to Vancouver as a piano virtuoso as &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;VRS audiences know him as a composer from his demonically difficult Toccata on L'homme arm ? and a short curtain-raiser commissioned for the its 40th anniversary . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; VRS brokers the Vancouver debut of the Schumann Quartet on March 1 as brothers Mark , Eric and Ken Schumann include violist Liisa Randalu in their family enterprise . The young quartet captured the 2016 Newcomer Award at the BBC Music Magazine Awards and hopes to captivate the VRS audience with Mozart&amp;rsquo ; s K. 499 &amp;ldquo ; Hoffmeister&amp;rdquo ; Quartet , Mendelssohn&amp;rsquo ; s Quartet in E minor , Op. 44 , No. 2 , and Shostakovich&amp;rsquo ; s intense Ninth Quartet ( 1964 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The programming of these three ensembles defines how chamber music ensembles are adapting for today&amp;rsquo ; s audiences . The Doric and Pavel Hass groups both seek to expand their range through the addition of guest artists and the inclusion of very recent work , belying the notion that chamber music audiences are stuck in the past . The Schumann ensemble is still establishing its reputation and wants to be heard in classics but , as younger performers , they consider the mid-century work of Shostakovich as &amp;ldquo ; classic&amp;rdquo ; as Mozart or Mendelssohn. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , there&amp;rsquo ; s a special quartet project at the end of March . Though local fans associate chamber music with the Vancouver Playhouse , presenters are always on the lookout for new spaces that are attractive , accessible , and acoustically good . On a golden day last fall a little delegation made a visit to the beautifully renovated Congregation Beth Israel synagogue on Oak Street . It didn&amp;rsquo ; t take long to confirm that this could be a wonderful new space for music as well as worship . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then it turned out that the Jerusalem Quartet , who have a long-standing invitation to town from the VRS &amp;mdash ; and who will be essaying all of the Beethoven string quartets in the fall &amp;mdash ; were coincidentally on the West Coast . Why not an impromptu concert ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Why not , indeed ? The band reported its second reunion in November 2019 , with two appearances at Coachella Valley Music and Arts Festival and other limited engagements . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The seven-month tour begins March 26 in El Paso , Texas , and ends on Sept. 12 in Vienna , Austria . " alt= " Singer Allie X attends the Schiaparelli Haute Couture Spring/Summer 2020 show as part of Paris Fashion Week on January 20 , 2020 in Paris , France . " data-portal-copyright= " Pascal Le Segretain " **28;28808;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206723 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Cape God&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Allie X&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Twin Music Inc&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The longer she lives in L.A. , the less Allie X resembles the Toronto singer that had a number of albums and EPs of pretty traditional pop tune smithing to her credit . Instead , Alexandra Ashley Hughes seems firmly entrenched in Tinseltown&amp;rsquo ; s goth-tinged electropop scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not surprising that YouTube channels such as &lt;a href= " https : //www.youtube.com/watch ? v=cw5iBgL ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;28838;TOOLONG have charted her musical shift toward a far more driving and less ballad-oriented approach since she started recording under the Allie X name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Songs such as Not So Bad in L.A. , Little Things and the chirpy voiced Girl of the Year from 2018&amp;rsquo ; s Super Sunset showcased a performer pursuing a kind of retro electronica vibe . She has totally hit that mark with the singles that dropped so far from Cape God . Fresh Laundry , Rings A Bell , Regulars , Love Me Wrong and Devil I Know all hone in on different aspects of her soprano range and the buzz around the release is building . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are five things to know about the album : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : Fresh Laundry&lt;/strong&gt; . With its big booming beat , spooky vocal and splashy keyboard hook , the opening tune to the 12 track record is a strong intro . The way the song builds to the point where the singer proclaims &amp;ldquo ; something has to die , &amp;rdquo ; and then just drops off into near silence before a keyboard hook that could be from a vintage UK nineties dance pop pulses her back into action is very nice indeed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Sarah Come Home&lt;/strong&gt; . There is something quite addictive about this heartfelt declaration to an absent girlfriend to return to the nest . What went wrong is never made clear , but the tune dances around on a hook that wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t sound out of place on a Carly Rae Jepsen record , which is high praise . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;28866;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;28902;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Super Duper Party People&lt;/strong&gt; . The Donna Summer-redux synthesizer line is hard to resist , but the rap-speak lyric is tragic . Plus , does anyone really think the elementary school rhyming of lines like we&amp;rsquo ; re super duper/we&amp;rsquo ; re the party troopers/uh-huh deserves to make it to a record ? It&amp;rsquo ; s a shockingly weak track on an album with a majority of solid ones . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : Guests&lt;/strong&gt; . Troye Sivan turns up on the bluesy ballad Love Me Wrong to wax romantically , while Mitski makes an appearance on Susie Save Your Love . This latter tune is the better of the two , with a slinky R&amp;amp ; B arrangement complete with a distorted guitar solo that sounds like it was recorded in a tin can . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Learning in Public&lt;/strong&gt; . The album closer shuffles out on a songs loaded with back up vocals , doo doo do do do&amp;rsquo ; s , a simple handclap and snare beat and reverberant piano plinks . It&amp;rsquo ; s both sparse and lush at the same time , which is a pretty brilliant production trick . Of course , the sound of the entire album is always pristine and carefully constructed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Allie X , March 27 , 8 p.m . Venue , 881 Granville . Tickets and info : &lt;a href= " https : **33;28938;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;28973;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Also out this week : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29001;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29037;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Gil **26;29073;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;We 're New Again : A Reimagining By Makaya McCraven&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;XL Recordings&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To mark the 10th anniversary of 2010s I&amp;rsquo ; m New Here , the final recording by the late , brilliant proto-rapper/poet/author Gil Scott-Heron , XL Recordings enlisted Chicago-based drummer and conceptualist McCraven to reimagine/reinterpret Heron&amp;rsquo ; s potent material . The result is quite a change from the original with the drummer often creating completely new jams that far outstrip the original . Case in point , New York is Killing Me , which mutates from a sample loop meditation into a swinging Afro-jazz spoken word piece that could have been on one of Heron&amp;rsquo ; s earlier seventies recordings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29101;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29137;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Orchid Ensemble&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;From A Dream&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;29173;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;29200;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The fourth album from this inventive and distinct trio of Lan Tung ( erhu/Chinese violin &amp;amp ; vocals ; Taiwan/Canada ) Dailin Hsieh ( zheng/Chinese zither ; Taiwan/Canada ) and Jonathan Bernard ( percussion ; Canada ) includes works by composers Dorothy Chang ( From A Dream ) , Jin Zhang ( No Rush , Fire ) and Farshid Samandari ( Ghosts of the Living ) and two traditional pieces . The title track is a stunner with multiple sections showcasing the band&amp;rsquo ; s command of their instruments to conjure up the sounds so many hear only in their heads . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; June 4-7 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Roundhouse Performance Hall , 181 Roundhouse Mews , and the Orpheum Annex , 823 Seymour , Sound of Dragon Music Festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //soundofdragon.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;29234;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29267;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29303;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Reverso ( Ryan Keberle/Frank Woeste/Vincent Courtois ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Melodic Line&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Out Note Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The second album from this excellent duo of American trombonist Ryan Keberle and French pianist Frank Woeste and cellist Vincent Courtois exploring compositions by the French composer&amp;rsquo ; s collective known as Les Six ( Georges Auric ( 1899&amp;ndash ; 1983 ) , Louis Durey ( 1888&amp;ndash ; 1979 ) , Arthur Honegger ( 1892&amp;ndash ; 1955 ) , Darius Milhaud ( 1892&amp;ndash ; 1974 ) , Francis Poulenc ( 1899&amp;ndash ; 1963 ) , and Germaine Tailleferre ( 1892&amp;ndash ; 1983 ) . Their music was a modernist reaction to impressionism , yet there are moments in the gorgeous chamber jazz of Exemplar that would make Debussy smile . The nine tracks straddle the space between classical and improvisational music and each player shines throughout . Courtois&amp;rsquo ; cello on Montparnasse is particularly moving as it seems to yearn for higher and higher notes on the neck . A positively beautiful session . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;29339;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29375;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Roy Forbes&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Edge of Blue&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;29411;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;29440;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On his 11th album , the Canadian folk fixture from Dawson Creek offers up another collection of soulful numbers that range from an almost La Bamba-esque early rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll sounds ( Don&amp;rsquo ; t Let Go ) , to beautiful country folk balladry ( The Beating of Your Very Own Heart ) and sweet blues ( Feeling Mighty Lonesome ) . As always , his quavering tenor is powerful and full of emotion , the playing is deep in the pocket and listening to the album leaves you wondering why he isn&amp;rsquo ; t a household name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 21 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Mel Lehan Hall , St. James , 3214 W. 10th Ave. , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; $32 at &lt;a href= " http : //roguefolk.bc.ca " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;29473;TOOLONG , Highlife Records , Tapestry Music &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;29504;TOOLONG " **36;29535;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;29573;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;29602;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 10 Feb 2020 19:12:07 **45;29633;TOOLONG ... soul singer Geoffroy lets his grief get worked through on 1952http : **36;29680;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;29718;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal artist Geoffroy channelled grief over his mother 's death into his new album . " alt= " Montreal artist Geoffroy channelled grief over his mother 's death into his new album . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;29754;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206809 " /&gt; **29;29784;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; **33;29815;TOOLONG : Feb. 15 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;29850;TOOLONG : The Fox Cabaret &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : $18 at &lt;a href= " https : //www.livenation.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;29886;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Montreal electro soul singer Geoffroy lost his mother in 2017 . He doesn&amp;rsquo ; t mince his words describing the effect her death had on him . Even after a lengthy illness , it was a jarring loss for the man born Geoffroy Sauve. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To work through his grief , he set about writing an album that explored the nooks and crannies of childhood memories , adult longings and &amp;mdash ; most important &amp;mdash ; the sense of place that parents provide . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Revisiting his youth in Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s **26;29916;TOOLONG ; ce , or NDG , neighbourhood in the city&amp;rsquo ; s West End , he penned the material that makes up his new album , 1952. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The title was the year of his mother&amp;rsquo ; s birth , and many songs relate directly back to her passing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was very close to my mother , and as an only child I stayed home until I was 27 , &amp;rdquo ; said Geoffroy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My work means I travel a lot and , when I was home , being close to my mom in her condition was important . She lived for a decade longer than was expected and that meant there was time to actually talk about death , about all the things you have on your chest , so there was luck in this bad luck . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29944;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29980;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The opening tune Fear of Falling Apart directly addresses personal loss . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over washes of vintage keyboards and distant echoing wooden percussion , the song outlines how hard the long demise of his mother was and its effects on him : &lt;em&gt;Got me scared of getting older / But my luck got stolen / Lost my mother to the devil , I 'll get by / But lost a part of my soul / And it feels like I 'm wandering off . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; About five or six songs on the album really are about the effects of her passing , with none as direct as the Fear of Falling Apart , &amp;rdquo ; Geoffroy said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The after-effects are there in When Everything Is Gone and Talking Low , but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t want anything to be directly evident or obvious for the listener who didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to go deeper . That&amp;rsquo ; s why I liked the name 1952 ; it&amp;rsquo ; s evocative without being obvious . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The captivating cover art , designed by Montreal tattoo artist Dan Climan and based on an old photo of Geoffroy&amp;rsquo ; s mother , also manages to be beautiful and evocative without being obvious . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30016;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30058;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;30094;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30125;TOOLONG ... latest music **25;30161;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finding the positive in the negative goes a long way to explaining how 1952 manages to possess a sonic stoicism and make you want to dance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That a former contestant on La Voix ( the Qu&amp;eacute ; bec franchise of The Voice TV singing competition ) would go on to make music as smart and non-obvious as what appears on 1952 is surprising . This critic would even consider the word stealthy to describe the way the songs work their way into your consciousness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Geoffroy stresses that he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t like being &amp;ldquo ; in your face&amp;rdquo ; as a person or in his music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His goal with 1952 was to grind through emotional issues and create emotionally reactive material . Judging from the reception to singles such as 21 Days or Woke Up Late , fans are hungry for more of Geoffroy&amp;rsquo ; s round , breathy vocals , classic instrumentation and tasty arrangements . 1952 features the same production teams as 2017&amp;rsquo ; s Polaris Prize long-listed debut Coastline . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That album garnered over 25 million streams and established Geoffroy as a bonafide star in Quebec and in Europe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now the mix of layered percussion , jazzy guitars , cool woodwinds and plentiful analog synthesizers and keyboards that Geoffroy , Gabriel Gagnon , Cl&amp;eacute ; ment Leduc and Max Gendron have crafted is getting its &amp;ldquo ; rest of Canada&amp;rdquo ; rollout . Fans may be surprised how the sounds aren&amp;rsquo ; t generated by pushing a button . This is live music performed by live musicians . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m not a computer geek and I don&amp;rsquo ; t engineer much , and I just love the warm , soft sounds that comes with using analog , &amp;rdquo ; Geoffroy said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the long way to go to get to the end product , but I think that you can hear when the results are reached with the digital shortcuts . I guess it&amp;rsquo ; s just another layer of professionalism , and I&amp;rsquo ; m a firm believer that if you are going to do it at all , do it right . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This opinion is shared by his studio collaborators . Leduc is a member of the touring band . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Having Cl&amp;eacute ; ment in the band allows us to easily export the sound we&amp;rsquo ; ve done in studio to the live arena , &amp;rdquo ; said Geoffroy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He brings along a big station and will play the Moog , the Prophet , the bass and as many other instruments as he can to make the music full . I usually bring a skeleton with ideas of where I want it to go to the guys , and then we flesh it out in studio and determine where it&amp;rsquo ; s really going to go . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Besides Leduc and Geoffroy , the band for this western Canadian tour also includes a live drummer . The singer says this configuration adds so much to the sound that he can&amp;rsquo ; t imagine doing it any other way . Larger shows are fleshed out to a five piece with an additional guitarist and vocalist. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the immediate draws in the music is that while it is soft , there are layers of active percussion throughout . Given the rabid embrace of all kinds of drum-crazy styles in the Quebec electronic scene , is it safe to say that Geoffroy raved in his youth ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Not really , I&amp;rsquo ; m just a big fan of all musics and genres , drawing inspiration from African and Haitian rhythms as well as deep house and even folkies , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Most of the material starts with a traditional singer-songwriter guitar and voice approach . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Geoffroy is &amp;ldquo ; happily surprised&amp;rdquo ; to see sell-out shows in markets across Canada where he hasn&amp;rsquo ; t performed before . Even though he chooses to write in English rather than French , there are challenges for Quebec artists to break into the rest of Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many of the excellent acts that are on the Bonsound label he records for have massive followings in their home province and across the world , but they couldn&amp;rsquo ; t sell out Saskatoon , let alone Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Streams are one thing , but no one has any idea how well things are working until you are actually selling tickets and going to those places , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can tell you that eight million streams for a new song won&amp;rsquo ; t fill my pocket , or anyone else&amp;rsquo ; s , once you split it all around . You have to tour . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Geoffroy will accept that the music on 1952 is informed by &amp;ldquo ; hard nostalgia , &amp;rdquo ; he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t agree that his music is brooding , pensive or sad . He says he&amp;rsquo ; s a happy , positive person who makes music that is ultimately intended to hit the listener in those places , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very important to differentiate softness , or melancholy , and sadness , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Damien Rice makes sad music . I don&amp;rsquo ; t. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30188;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30230;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? " alt= " The iconic Janet Jackson will perform at Vancouver 's Rogers Arena this August . " data-portal-copyright= " Francois Nel " **28;30712;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1211520 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The iconic Janet Jackson &amp;ndash ; Miss Jackson if you&amp;rsquo ; re nasty &amp;ndash ; will bring her Black Diamond World Tour to Vancouver in August . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The tour will feature all-new production , new music from her upcoming album Black Diamond and , of course , a steady stream of greatest hits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jackson&amp;rsquo ; s outing comes as her iconic Rhythm Nation 1814 recently celebrated its 30th anniversary in September 2019. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Black Diamond tour will kick off in late June in Miami and make its way across the U.S. and Canada , with European dates to be announced at a later time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jackson , who is also the sister of late King of Pop Michael Jackson , is one of the most influential figures in pop history and dance , with two Grammy Awards , two Emmy nominations , a Golden Globe Award and an Academy Award nomination , along with dozens of American Music Awards , MTV Video Music Awards and Billboard Music Awards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jackson will perform at Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Rogers Arena on Aug. 22 , 2020 , with tickets on sale this Thursday through &lt;a href= " https : //www.livenation.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;LiveNation&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " mailto:sip@postmedia.com " **31;30742;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;30775;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;30802;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;30835;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;30871;TOOLONG Stephanie IpMon , 10 Feb 2020 16:27:08 **45;30907;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicFive things to know about U kin B the Sun , by Frazey Fordhttp : **36;30954;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;30992;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver singer/songwriter Frazey Ford . " alt= " Vancouver singer/songwriter Frazey Ford . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31028;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200781 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;U kin B the Sun&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Frazey Ford&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Arts &amp; Crafts&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s been six years since Indian Ocean . The second album by former Be Good Tanyas singer Frazey Ford found the Vancouver-based soul/folksinger pairing her instantly recognizable voice with backing from no less than the Hi Rhythm Section , the Reverend Al Green&amp;rsquo ; s band . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was a perfect fit for her unique delivery , which includes the kind of drawled diction and half-finished phrasing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like blues or R&amp;amp ; B greats , it&amp;rsquo ; s all about the feel as much as the meaning in the lyrics , although hits such as Done from Indian Ocean hit with both groove and message about having had enough of the dysfunction and violence in society . The same is true of The Kids are Having None of it on the new album , which holds out hope for the younger generation fixing the mess that is the world today . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Once again , the music is in a classic &amp;rsquo ; 70s soul vein and works its way into your brain slowly . Here are five things to know about the album : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : Azad&lt;/strong&gt; . The opening song is proof of how less is more in the world of pop music . Rolling in on a superb bass line and simple snare and hi-hat beat , the tune slowly builds the whole way through , gaining simple organ , smooth backing vocals and then a funky-as-can-be keyboard vamp right out of Stevie Wonder&amp;rsquo ; s Superstitious . It&amp;rsquo ; s a killer first tune . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : Money Ca n't Buy&lt;/strong&gt; . &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; ve got a life that money can&amp;rsquo ; t buy . &amp;rdquo ; Ford repeats this line repeatedly , almost chanting it , as she outlines how the subject of the song has blown it again . Presumably , this is due to hubris . But it&amp;rsquo ; s not an angry song as much as a regretful statement about someone you sense that she cares/cared about . It&amp;rsquo ; s very moody . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Let 's Start Again&lt;/strong&gt; . This could be the most straight-ahead , blues tinged tune Ford has ever recorded . A loving plea to do as the title declares , the song is pleading for more . But , ultimately , it ends on a descending piano scale that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t sound hopeful at all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : The Kids are Having None of it&lt;/strong&gt; . Ford notes in the news release for the album that the album&amp;rsquo ; s songs came out in a much more direct way than her previous work , and it&amp;rsquo ; s far more direct . Nowhere is this more clear than in this single where she sounds like she is warning those in power to take notice of what the future may hold if they don&amp;rsquo ; t listen to the youth of today . The singer mentions being &amp;ldquo ; more in touch with the wholeness of anger , &amp;rdquo ; and this track certainly is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Golden&lt;/strong&gt; . One of the most upbeat songs of the set , U kin B the Sun might have benefited from a few more like this song to shuffle up the overarching mood of resignation and melancholy that pervades the 11 tracks . Sometimes , being able to punch air and be &amp;ldquo ; happy to jump in the sea&amp;rdquo ; over a sweet guitar solo really is enough . This is sure to be an in-concert hit when Ford hits the road again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;31058;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;31094;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Also out this week : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Alabaster DePlume&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;To Cy and Lee : Instrumentals Vol. 1&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; International Anthem&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; London-based singer/instrumentalist DePlume collects 11 instrumentals recorded over the past eight years onto one standout album . Opening with the gorgeous Visit Croatia , featuring a haunting clarinet soaring over scant piano and echoing keyboards , the album ventures into terrain as different as Song of the Foundling , which sounds like Tinariwen interpreted by avant-garde classical musicians , to Why , Buzzardman , Why , with its classical piano flourishes . An unusual , and completely rewarding , record whose only shortcoming is a bit too much tempo consistency in the writing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **33;31130;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;31165;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Last Wild Sons&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;East Side of Midnight&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;31201;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;31236;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From 1987 to 1991 Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Last Wild Sons were regulars on the exploding roots-rock scene . Brothers Gene , Darren and Shaun ( Butch ) Murphy and buddy Al Black Davidson formed Version 1 of this band and recorded one album that saw its release and a collection of other tracks . These were consigned to the closet for a few decades , but are finally getting out there and it&amp;rsquo ; s crazy that songs such as the excellent , chiming Tell Em What I Was and title track didn&amp;rsquo ; t hit back in the day . If you like bands like the Blasters , X or Jason and the Scorchers , you will want to hear this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;31267;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;31303;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sons of Apollo&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;31339;TOOLONG &lt;em&gt;Inside Out Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Supergroups usually don&amp;rsquo ; t live up to their billing . However , the pairing of former Dream Theatre members Mike Portnoy and Derek Sherinian with Ron ( Bumblefoot ) Thal ( ex-Guns N Roses ) , Billy Sheehan ( ex-David Lee Roth ) and Jeff Scott Soto ( ex-Journey ) appears to be one that delivers the kind of progressive metal that Dream Theatre were beloved for . If you want complex-but-heavy workouts full of plenty of solos , then King of Delusion , or the nearly 16-minutes-long New World Today , won&amp;rsquo ; t disappoint . But it&amp;rsquo ; s also pretty accessible on other tracks , explaining why the album debuted at No. 1 on the Billboard Current Hard Rock chart . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;31374;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;31410;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tami Neilson&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Chickaboom ! &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Outside Music&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cover declares &amp;ldquo ; the hot rockin&amp;rsquo ; lady of country , rockabilly and soul sings &amp;hellip ; &amp;rdquo ; And she does . From the slinky opener Call Your Mama , with its constant cowbell and percussion driving home the message that the fella can go home as his days of being a useless mooch are over , to the raucous jam of Tell Me that You Love Me , Neilson lives up to her mile-high bouffant and fringe-jacket &amp;rsquo ; 50s vibe . Great production , particularly on the Everly Brothers-esque closer Sleep . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;31446;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;31481;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;31517;TOOLONG " **36;31548;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;31586;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;31615;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 07 Feb 2020 19:07:55 **45;31646;TOOLONG ... announcement : The Rolling Stones play B.C. ; margin-top:8px ; overflow:hidden ; padding:8px 0 7px ; text-align:center ; text-overflow:ellipsis ; white-space:nowrap ; " &gt;A post shared by &lt;a href= " https : **33;32050;TOOLONG ... style= " &lt;time style= " font-family:Arial , sans-serif ; font-size:14px ; line-height:17px ; " datetime= " **25;32085;TOOLONG " &gt;Feb 3 , 2020 at 3:52pm **45;32112;TOOLONG ... &lt;script async src= " **28;32159;TOOLONG " &gt;&lt;/sc ... &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s always a pleasure to return to North America and play for some of the biggest and best crowds in the world , &amp;rdquo ; said the 76-year-old Jagger , who had surgery to replace a heart valve last April . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets go on sale Friday at 10 a.m . Prices haven&amp;rsquo ; t yet been released . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fans can register for presale at &lt;a href= " https : **33;32189;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Rollingstones.com. &lt;/a&gt; ... &lt;/p&gt; Scott BrownFri , 07 Feb 2020 06:53:03 **45;32224;TOOLONG ... review : 5 things to know about Temples and Art d'Ecco at the Foxhttp : **37;32271;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32310;TOOLONG ... title= " Temples . UK psychedelic pop band 2017 . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Temples . UK psychedelic pop band 2017 . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32346;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206810 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Temples and Art d'Ecco&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;32376;TOOLONG : Feb. 4 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;32411;TOOLONG : Fox Cabaret , 2321 Main &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kettering , UK , quartet Temples has six years of touring and recording under its belt now and it shows . The group&amp;rsquo ; s performance at the Fox Cabaret on a snowy/rainy Vancouver night was as polished and paced as the latest single Holy Horses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Adding to the buzz around the gig was the presence of rising gulf island act &lt;a href= " http : //artdeccomusic.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Art d'Ecco&lt;/a&gt; , whose album Tresspasser is an absolute joy of glammed-up swagger . Having played a number of high profile gigs such as the Squamish Music Festival , the singer and his tight backing band are really taking shape . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are five things to know about the show : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 : The Howl&lt;/strong&gt; . The band opened with the fourth tune from its most recent album , Hot Motion . With its fuzzed out lead riff , airy keyboards ( courtesy of rhythm guitarist Adam Smith ) and chanting vocal hook , the song is a perfect example of what the band does right . Blending classic &amp;rsquo ; 60s style psychedelic pop with some flourishes that could only come from players who lived through the Brit Pop era , the tune is a pulsing gem . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 : You 're Either on Something&lt;/strong&gt; . One of the best songs the band has written to date just slays live . With its chiming harmonies and rising break , it is totally suited to lead **27;32447;TOOLONG James Bagshaw&amp;rsquo ; s high voice . Straight and to the point . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 : Holy Horses&lt;/strong&gt; . And this could be the second best song the band has produced . It&amp;rsquo ; s like a snarling mix of Beatles and Oasis with a bouncing bass line broken up by one of the hardest rocking hooks in the group&amp;rsquo ; s repertoire . At a time when lengthy guitar jams are &amp;mdash ; fairly &amp;mdash ; consigned to the jam band heap , Temples will still take an occasional workout and let their trippy hair fly . They did a nice job on doing that on this one , with Bagshaw laying down some razor sharp leads . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;32476;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;32512;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 : The drums&lt;/strong&gt; . The band is on its third drummer , and they may still not have the right one . Rens Ottink did a great job keeping time and keeping it really thudding and straight-ahead . The thing is , the songs could stand a bit of breathing room that a more light touch could bring . Temples leans much more to the pop side than the rock side on a lot of the material and the four-on-the-floor pounding might be holding back the songs from soaring . Let Ottink swing a bit and see what happens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 : Art d'Ecco&lt;/strong&gt; . It&amp;rsquo ; s always great to see a local warm-up act really bring it . With a set of solid songs , good stage look and vibe and a really fine singing voice , d&amp;rsquo ; Ecco made Temples have to bring its A-game . Veering from glam rock to some jumpy dance pop , the sound is seductive , if not quite as outrageous as it could be . Fans are looking forward to hearing the next album . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;32548;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;32584;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;32620;TOOLONG " **36;32651;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;32689;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;32718;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynThu , 06 Feb 2020 02:05:18 **45;32749;TOOLONG ... Black Halos rock back to back nights at the Rickshawhttp : **32;32796;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32830;TOOLONG ... title= " The Black Halos play Rickshaw Theatre on Feb. 7 and 8. " alt= " The Black Halos play Rickshaw Theatre on Feb. 7 and 8. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32866;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200581 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Black Halos&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;32896;TOOLONG : Feb. 7 , 8 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;32931;TOOLONG : Rickshaw Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : $20 , at &lt;a href= " https : **33;32967;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; ticketweb.ca&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are fake mall punks . There are genuine gutter punks . Billy Hopeless knows where The Black Halos fans hail from . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Black Halos is a real outsider rock n&amp;rsquo ; roll band that speaks for , and to , people who don&amp;rsquo ; t fit ; people like me. &amp;rdquo ; said Hopeless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very human band putting forth real emotion , real humanity , because that is what all good rock n&amp;rsquo ; roll was always like . All the boy bands I grew up listening to &amp;mdash ; Dead Boys , The Boyz , The Ducky Boys , even the Ramones and the New York Dolls &amp;mdash ; were always on the outside looking in. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;33002;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;33038;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; From 1993 to 2008 , Hopeless and the initial core of guitarists Rich Jones and Jay Millette , drummer Rob Zgaljic , and assorted bassists toured relentlessly . The hard work paid off as the band was justly recognized for its high-energy live shows and solid , swaggering songwriting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Inking a deal with Seattle&amp;rsquo ; s Sup Pop label , the band&amp;rsquo ; s second album The Violent Years ( 2001 ) was produced by grunge era legend Jack Endino ( Nirvana , Soundgarden ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The album was well received . Things were looking up . Then they weren&amp;rsquo ; t. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Rich and I had a falling out , and he went off to join Amen , and maybe that was the start of a slow implosion , &amp;rdquo ; said Hopeless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We put out Alive Without Control ( 2005 ) with Jay and Rob and guitarist Davey French and Kelly Wheeler ( bass ) , and people just loved it , and the one that followed with a different lineup ( We Are Not Alone , 2007 ) , too . But when our gear and trailer got stolen in Montreal , it was the final straw . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band broke-up in 2008 , and appeared consigned to the history books . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Millette moved to Toronto and played in Darlings of Chelsea . Jones relocated to England and is a member of former Hanoi Rocks&amp;rsquo ; singer Michael Moore&amp;rsquo ; s band , gigging all over Europe . Zgaljic switched from playing to concert production . Hopeless formed The Bonitos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nothing felt quite like the Halos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We took advice from Joey from DOA , breaking up and re-forming with different line ups here and there , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then Rich and I did a tour in Spain a few years back with this group the Basque Street Boys as our band , and it went over so great that we started writing new songs . Rich moved to Toronto and kept running into Jay and asking him about rejoining , and one drunken night he said yes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Adding in former Age of Electric bassist John Kearns and drummer Danni Action , the reformed band went back into the studio with Endino to record the new Geisterbahn 7&amp;Prime ; EP and digital single . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The three song recording includes the air-punching Geisterbahn II , Swell Maps-like Tandem Drown , and full-on punk attack of Fossil Fuel . It&amp;rsquo ; s a definite return to form for the band , with all of the spit and sweat that fans would expect . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was me and Rich doing Spain that really cemented it , playing to 2,000-plus people singing along to every word when we opened for Danzig , &amp;rdquo ; said Hopeless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; People kept asking about whether we were going to come back ; in Spain , in the States , and at home in Vancouver . It kind of gets you thinking , &amp;ldquo ; maybe we weren&amp;rsquo ; t s&amp;ndash ; t and were pretty good . &amp;rdquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;33074;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;33116;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;33152;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;33183;TOOLONG ... latest music **25;33219;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;33246;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;33282;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33313;TOOLONG ... title= " Black Halos live in Barcelona . " alt= " Black Halos live in Barcelona . " data-portal-copyright= " Picasa " **28;33349;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202676 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hopeless says the band has written a whole album and hopes to head into studio sometime in 2020 to record it . That album will be out on Yeah Right ! Records run by London , ON. , musician Tony Lima . Home to bands from the Thunder Queens to The Spitfires , the label also put out the very first Black Halos single . Hopeless says it&amp;rsquo ; s like being back home again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new material is coming across the way Hopeless says the best songs always did , from a process of collaboration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can write a song that is really catchy , happy and poppy , and Rich will put something on it that makes it down and depressing sounding , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then I&amp;rsquo ; ll complain , and he&amp;rsquo ; ll come up with some really happy melody that just makes it all so right . It&amp;rsquo ; s always been that way with our best songs . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Back in the band&amp;rsquo ; s heyday , the Brickyard , the Niagara and the Piccadilly Pub were pillars of the local rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll revival . The Black Halos held court regularly , as did such gutter rockers as the Murder City Devils , The Supersuckers , and others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band was well-known for championing the local scene , and Hopeless still sounds like a cheerleader discussing the killer line up of support acts for the coming back-to-back shows at Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s present rock palace ; the Rickshaw. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These were the guys I wanted , these were the bands I liked , &amp;rdquo ; said Hopeless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I remember the Spitfires giving us their first demo tape at the Niagara . I&amp;rsquo ; ve known Greg from Bishops Green going all the way back to the Subway Thugs , and he can really write a song &amp;mdash ; his craftsmanship is incredible . Sore Points just got back from touring in Thailand and areas , and Chain Whip kicks ass . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only band that couldn&amp;rsquo ; t be set up for the bill was Alien Boys . No doubt , the chance will come somewhere down the road . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hopeless notes that the Black Halos are ready to go . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The scene seemed to be drifting for awhile , but it always does that , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It goes through phases where everyone is staring at their shoes until someone goes &amp;lsquo ; hey , look up here&amp;rsquo ; . Then they do , and there are killer bands like Amyl and the Sniffers from Australia , and a bunch of others for a bit , and then everyone starts looking at their shoes again . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It goes without saying that Hopeless intends to be around for as many of those cycles as come around in his lifetime . The Black Halos are a favourite vehicle to deliver his style of bark and snarl singing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve got a booking agent now , we want to get this album done , and then we&amp;rsquo ; ll see , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And the day will undoubtedly come when I&amp;rsquo ; ll be sitting in a hotel room going , &amp;lsquo ; I hate this&amp;rsquo ; once more . Then you get on stage and it&amp;rsquo ; s all forgotten , because that&amp;rsquo ; s what we live for . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The Black Halos plays with The Spitfires and Sore Points Feb. 7 and with Bishops Green and Chain Whip on Feb. 8. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;33379;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;33421;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? " alt= " The sets are pure comic book whimsy for Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . " data-portal-copyright= " David Cooper " **28;33976;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206415 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Barber of Seville&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 13 , 15 , 20 , 23 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Queen Elizabeth Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets &amp; info : &lt;/strong&gt; From $41.75 at &lt;a href= " https : //www.vancouveropera.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;34006;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gioachino Rossini&amp;rsquo ; s 1816 The Barber of Seville is beyond popular ; it&amp;rsquo ; s ubiquitous . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Everyone has heard bits of Rossini&amp;rsquo ; s sparkling opera buffa score in everything from TV adverts to Bugs Bunny cartoons . Do we need another staged production ? Well , yes , we do . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even at just 24 , when he penned the world&amp;rsquo ; s favourite comic opera , Rossini was the consummate master of his craft . The farcical plot stuffed with comic archetypes never fails to amuse and cries out for constant creative reinvention : a perfect opportunity for cast , director , and designer to be original while having fun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver Opera interim general director Tom Wright told me this latest Barber has been in the works for almost two years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This production was conceived 18 months ago , when I asked Ashlie Corcoran to consider directing it , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;nbsp ; Wright said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The choice of Corcoran , artistic director of the Arts Club Theatre , is in keeping with VO&amp;rsquo ; s mandate to work with younger Canadian talents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have a young and very diverse cast , &amp;rdquo ; says Wright , and that includes a bumper crop of VO debuts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34039;TOOLONG ... title= " California baritone Edward Nelson sings Figaro in Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . Photo credit : Arielle Doneson. " alt= " California baritone Edward Nelson sings Figaro in Vancouver Opera 's Barber of Seville . Photo credit : Arielle Doneson. " data-portal-copyright= " Arielle Doneson " **28;34075;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206414 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In his first Vancouver appearance , California baritone ( barihunk ? ) Edward Nelson sings Figaro and VO favourite mezzo-soprano Julie Boulianne is his amorata , Rosina. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wright first heard Taehyun Jun , making his VO debut as Don Basilio , in an audition where he was singing Rossini , and immediately decided that the Korean-born baritone was a &amp;ldquo ; must have&amp;rdquo ; for this production . Conductor Nathan Brock , formerly principal conductor of the Hamburgische Staatsoper , is another VO first-timer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Opera aficionados will take note of all these details , but the most striking initial delight is the visual style of the production . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wright explained that no matter how much a company like Vancouver Opera might want to do productions from scratch with purpose-built sets and costumes , that&amp;rsquo ; s not how creating opera works these days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This Barber was conceived for Victoria&amp;rsquo ; s Pacific Opera , with a design team that included Ken MacDonald , perhaps best known here for his stunning visual conception for The Overcoat . If you are expecting a sugary evocation of 18th-century Seville , think again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The look of the production is quite unusual , &amp;rdquo ; says Wright . &amp;ldquo ; I would describe as it comic book whimsy. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is one rather important distinction between Victoria &amp;mdash ; which mounts shows in the bijou Royal Theatre &amp;mdash ; and the grand open spaces of our mid-century modern Queen Elizabeth theatre , a stage that can overwhelm the unwary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Comedy on a stage the size of the Queen Elizabeth&amp;rsquo ; s is not easy , &amp;rdquo ; admits Wright . &amp;ldquo ; I find myself telling everyone to make it as intimate as possible while being as broad as possible ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wright says the show has already travelled to big halls in Winnipeg and Calgary , with good results . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wright warns that the world of opera , like everything else , is changing . Some surefire hits of yesteryear can&amp;rsquo ; t be relied on today . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Works that have always sold well aren&amp;rsquo ; t doing that any more , &amp;rdquo ; he notes . He remains confident , however , that Barber should be an exception . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Ticket sales are very strong . If you have never seen an opera , this is the one to start with ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Barber should also have multi-generational appeal , and VO&amp;rsquo ; s practice of adding a matinee into the performance schedule provides extra opportunities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While afternoon performances aren&amp;rsquo ; t exactly new in the opera world , they do seem to increasingly appeal to patrons , who particularly like the relative informality of afternoon performances , plus being able to get home for dinner while it is still daylight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Matinees are a particular boon for fans who want to share their passion for opera with younger relations and friends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our matinee performances have been surprisingly popular , &amp;rdquo ; says Wright . &amp;ldquo ; And we want to push the idea of grandparents bringing the new generation along for a special treat . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;34105;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;34147;TOOLONG ... about Vancouver **26;34183;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;34211;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;34247;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;34275;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsMusicConcert review : Sinead O'Connor blows away a sold-out Vogue Theatrehttp : **33;34496;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34531;TOOLONG ... title= " Sinead O'Connor . Tour photo 2020. " alt= " Sinead O'Connor . Tour photo 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34567;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1206546 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Sinead O'Connor&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;34597;TOOLONG : Feb. 2 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;34632;TOOLONG : Vogue Theatre , 918 Granville &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not many artists arrive on an album as truly unique as The Lion and the Cobra . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Released in 1987 , Irish singer Sinead O&amp;rsquo ; Connor&amp;rsquo ; s debut inhabited a space all its own . Mixing folk , rock , pop and cutting-edge electronica of the day with her thunderous voice , songs such as Mandinka , Troy and I Want ( Your Hands On Me ) sound fresh to this very day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her next album &amp;mdash ; 1990&amp;rsquo ; s I Do Not Want What I Haven&amp;rsquo ; t Got &amp;mdash ; cemented her place in pop history . After that , she was as likely to be in the press or public eye for what she said and did than what she produced musically . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not that she didn&amp;rsquo ; t continue to produce often fine records , many of which appeared to be familiar to the fans who packed the Vogue to see O&amp;rsquo ; Connor&amp;rsquo ; s first local appearance here in decades . She got a standing ovation before she even strolled on stage , smiling shyly in her black hijab and long-sleeved , floor-length gown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The singer converted to Islam in October 2018 , changing her name to Shuhada Davitt . She still records and tours under her previous moniker , which makes rather a lot of sense . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34668;TOOLONG ... title= " Sinead O'Connor . Tour photo 2020. " alt= " Sinead O'Connor . Tour photo 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34704;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1205752 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Opening with her cover of John Grant&amp;rsquo ; s Queen of Denmark from 2012&amp;rsquo ; s How About I Be Me ( And You Be You ) , O&amp;rsquo ; Connor was in excellent voice all night . Backed by a crew that could lay down everything from the blue-eyed soul-pop of songs such as Harbour or Hold Back the Night from 2014&amp;rsquo ; s I&amp;rsquo ; m Not Bossy , I&amp;rsquo ; m the Boss to the punky directness of The Emperor&amp;rsquo ; s New Clothes or the still-jarring Black Boys on Mopeds from I Do Not Want What I Haven&amp;rsquo ; t Got . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This former song is all the more relevant today in the days of #blacklivesmatter and the increasing incidence of racist attacks in post-Brexit Britain . Proving that she really was far ahead of the curve , the line about millions of people telling her how to live her life in The Emperor&amp;rsquo ; s New Clothes better describes today&amp;rsquo ; s social media trolling society than it did the press attacks of the 1990s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another showstopper was the brilliant 4th and Vine from How About I Be Me ( And You Be You ) , which recounts the story of a junkie who knows it&amp;rsquo ; s time to quit , but can&amp;rsquo ; t . With the opioid crisis rampant in the city&amp;rsquo ; s streets , the song&amp;rsquo ; s powerful lyrics rang all too true . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mixing material from throughout her career , although sadly nothing from her fine traditional 2002 folk album Sean-N&amp;oacute ; s Nua , there were some duds. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some of the love songs from her more recent records could fit comfortably on a Celine Dion record or some other middle-of-the-road artist . But everything was performed with passion and presence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Connor is in her 50s now , and the majority of the audience was within 20 years or so of that mark . It meant that songs such as This Is the Last Day of Our Acquaintance gets a different reaction as its story of divorce is pretty familiar to &amp;mdash ; statistically &amp;mdash ; 50 per cent or more of the crowd . And walking out of the performance , a line from Black Boys on Mopeds kept replaying in this reviewer&amp;rsquo ; s head : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;These are dangerous days , to say what you feel is to dig your own grave . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Good on Sinead to still be able to resonate and evaluate the world around us . She is at times difficult , at times baffling , but also incredibly talented . Having her return to touring and performing is welcomed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And , yes , Nothing Compares 2 U is still fantastic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;34734;TOOLONG " **36;34765;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;34803;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;34832;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;34863;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ?
