Michael Bolton , soulful singing with symphonies a dream come truehttp : **36;35006;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35044;TOOLONG ... title= " Michael Bolton brings The Symphony Sessions to the Orpheum Theatre on Feb. 12. " alt= " Michael Bolton brings The Symphony Sessions to the Orpheum Theatre on Feb. 12. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35080;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200595 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Michael Bolton : The Symphony Sessions&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;35110;TOOLONG : Feb. 12 , 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;35145;TOOLONG : Orpheum Theatre , 601 Smithe St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : From $44.50 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;35181;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;35216;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Michael Bolton celebrates 50 years in the music business this year . The 66-year-old vocalist says it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to believe it when he reads it in his own press kit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fans would be surprised to know he had his beginnings in the hard rock and heavy metal scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was signed to Epic Records when I was 16 , for a singles deal , and a year later received a kind note from them that I was now free to sign a deal with whoever I liked , as they no longer required my services , &amp;rdquo ; said Bolton . &amp;ldquo ; So that was my first positive experience in the industry , and I kept getting signed to different companies for one or two solo albums and then it was over , or with the band Blackjack for two albums and it was over . It was a blur of excited executives who saw a lot of potential in the marketing metrics , but me not having a hit . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Blackjack&amp;rsquo ; s peak was once opening for Ozzy Osbourne . Bolton started to peak when he co-wrote How Am I Supposed to Live Without You with pop singer Laura Branigan . The tune hit No. 1 on the Adult Contemporary chart in 1983 , holding the position for three weeks . Bolton would follow up this success with the song I Found Someone . A minor hit for Branigan in 1985 , the tune put Cher back on the charts two years later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everything changed with How Am I Supposed to Live Without You , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Record companies and managers all look at you differently because you laid the golden egg , and they want to be there for the next one . And I was hungry for that next one . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fittingly , his breakout 1987 album was titled The Hunger . Featuring Bolton&amp;rsquo ; s cover version of Otis Redding&amp;rsquo ; s classic ( Sittin&amp;rsquo ; On ) the Dock of the Bay , the album was certified double platinum in the U.S. After half a life in the industry , Bolton had arrived . The stylistic change from howler to crooner has translated into in excess of 75 million in album sales , two Billboard #1 singles , eight top-10 albums , six American Music Awards and two Grammy Awards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It just took Bolton a bit longer than the mythical 10,000 hours of work before he hit the jackpot. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It took 34 years of life before I had my own hit as an artist , so I was off by 18 years , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Now I&amp;rsquo ; ve got the new album on Entertainment One titled A Symphony of Hits . That&amp;rsquo ; s how it goes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;35248;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;35284;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Recorded with the 65-piece West Australian Philharmonic Orchestra in Perth , 2019&amp;rsquo ; s A Symphony of Hits presents Bolton&amp;rsquo ; s powerhouse pipes with his band and the full orchestra covering some of his most-loved songs , including When a Man Loves a Woman , To Love Somebody and the career-shifting How Am I Supposed to Live Without You . Known for blasting out his vocals , he sounds more laid back and in the pocket on the recording than on many of his other adult contemporary releases . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love the orchestrations so much , singing with an orchestra , and it all goes back to being invited to sing with Luciano Pavarotti , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to just come out and sing a pop song , I wanted to sing in his world . So I started studying opera , staying up late listening to Caruso and Pavarotti and others I didn&amp;rsquo ; t really know and I learned you need a very different engine to hold a high C over multiple bars . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Operatic technique came pretty naturally to Bolton , and he continued studying to learn techniques to protect and project his voice . The end result of the preparation was that it widened his appreciation for the music and other ways his mix of R&amp;amp ; B , pop and country classics and originals could be presented . The arrangements he and his band will perform with the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra came out of experimenting with ways of &amp;ldquo ; freshening up the sets . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is nothing quite as powerful and moving as a full orchestra , and we were able to time the recording with the beginning of an Australian tour , so I was able to get everything done and hit the road with a full orchestra every night , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; While there are many other great orchestras to work with , you really need to have one that can cut a deal with you where you retain the use of your orchestrations to be able to tour all over the world . Otherwise , you may be limited to doing only a few , specialized limited engagements or TV specials . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bolton knows a few things about how to produce a good TV special . Michael Bolton&amp;rsquo ; s Big , Sexy Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day Special on Netflix showcased comedy chops that nobody knew the singer possessed until appearing in a Saturday Night Live collaboration with The Lonely Island titled Jack Sparrow . That digital short became a viral sensation and the singer has described the segment in interviews as &amp;ldquo ; the gift that keeps on giving . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;35320;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;35356;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You go on Saturday Night Live and get embraced by a new audience , and the next thing you know , you are embraced by a whole new public of 12- to 40-year-olds who have never owned one of your albums , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; And then you have a whole new fan base buying your albums and coming to your shows that wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have existed without Captain Jack Sparrow . It was fun from the moment I met Andy Samberg and the Lonely Island are just unstoppably funny together in a room to work with . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Besides singing with symphonies and going for laughs on SNL and Netflix , Bolton has also taken on a new role for this coming tour : perfumer . His &amp;ldquo ; soulful fragrance collection&amp;rdquo ; includes three scents : Time , Love and Tenderness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;T&lt;/span&gt; his three-piece fragrance collection captures the essence of his eponymous album and hit single of the same name , &amp;rdquo ; says the press release , noting the soulful top notes , passionate heart notes and romantic dry down . All in a day&amp;rsquo ; s marketing , says a laughing Bolton : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What else could we sell ? How about perfume , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; But really , scents have become something tied to artists and there is a woman who does that show Living who thought it would be a great fit to sell it at the shows and online . And the fans love it , so there it is. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Expect to hear a selection of songs that would pair perfectly with each of those scents when Bolton and the VSO perform . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;35392;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;35423;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;35461;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;35490;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;35521;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35563;TOOLONG ... review : Sinead O'Connor blows away a sold out Vogue **28;35598;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35628;TOOLONG ... got you covered on the Vancouver music **26;35663;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **31;35691;TOOLONG " &; ... Things to Do in Metro Vancouver Feb.7-Feb 13 : Straight White Men , Luna , and morehttp : **36;35895;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35933;TOOLONG ... title= " Ellen Close and Praneet Akilla star in Cipher , a new Canadian mystery at Granville Island Stage Feb. 6-March 7. " data-portal-copyright= " David Cooper " **28;35969;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202412 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whether you&amp;rsquo ; re looking for date ideas , free things to do or just something fun to do in downtown Vancouver , you can&amp;rsquo ; t go wrong with our list of events happening around Metro Vancouver between Feb.7-Feb 13. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Headlining this week&amp;rsquo ; s picks is the Vertigo Theatre/Arts Club co-production of Cipher. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For more ideas , click &lt;a href= " https : **33;35999;TOOLONG ... for our coverage of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s arts scene , or &lt;a href= " http : **34;36034;TOOLONG ... to search our entertainment listings database . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are seven things to do in Metro Vancouver this week : &lt;/p&gt; **27;36070;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 6-March 7 , various times &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Granville Island Stage &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $29 , at &lt;a href= " https : **33;36099;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;36134;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Winner of the 2018 Alberta Playwriting Competition , Cipher is based on a ( fictional ) 63-year-old Vancouver Island cold case . A forensic toxicologist investigates , along with a man who has a personal stake in the mystery of the Beacon Hill body ( Ellen Close and Praneet Akilla are pictured ) . The Vertigo Theatre/Arts Club co-production unveils the plot via a traditional , text-based approach and contemporary dance and movement sequences . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36162;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36198;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;A Comedy Show for Ryan&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;36228;TOOLONG : Feb. 9 at 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Yuk Yuk&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $20 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;36263;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;36298;TOOLONG ... , $25 at the door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ryan Lachance ( pictured ) lives with spastic quadriplegic cerebral palsy , the most severe type of CP . But Lachance has overcome his challenge to become a beloved and popular standup come on the local comedy circuit . Unfortunately , he&amp;rsquo ; s been unable to work owing to a severe infection . To raise funds for their fellow comic&amp;rsquo ; s much-needed medical supplies , local comedians are banding together for a fundraiser . The lineup includes Sophia Johnson , Ryan Gunther , Desmond Williams , Gavin Clarkson , Julia Stretch , Nic Enright-Morin , and Charles Demers . One hundred per cent of the ticket proceeds go directly to Lachance. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36331;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36367;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Straight White Men&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 6-15 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Gateway Theatre , Richmond &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $29 , at &lt;a href= " https : **33;36397;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;36432;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Theatregoers who enjoyed SpeakEasy Theatre&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;36464;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;2019 production of Young Jean Lee 's The Shipment&lt;/a&gt; ( not to mention anyone who enjoys the spectacle of privileged white men goofing off and getting real ) will want to check out Straight White Men . Directed by Chelsea Haberlin ( ITSAZOO ) and Fay Nass ( Frank Theatre ) , the play is another work by Lee but instead of race , the playwright/button-pusher has set her sights on white privilege and class . The production marks the Canadian debut of the play , which was the first Broadway play by a female Asian-American playwright . A &amp;ldquo ; Talk Forward&amp;rdquo ; with a special guest accompanies each performance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36499;TOOLONG ... title= " Picture credit : Luz Gallardo. " alt= " Picture credit : Luz Gallardo. " data-portal-copyright= " Luz Gallardo " **28;36535;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202069 " /&gt; **25;36565;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 13 at 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Venue &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $30 , at &lt;a href= " https : **33;36592;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;36627;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Luna hasn&amp;rsquo ; t been all that active lately ; the band has mostly been recording covers ( Roy Orbison&amp;rsquo ; s California Blue , for example ) for limited-release singles . But most fans of the &amp;lsquo ; 90s indie-pop group want to hear the old songs anyway , and will no doubt embrace Dean Wareham and co. &amp;rsquo ; s decision to hit the road to play the entirety of 1995&amp;rsquo ; s Penthouse . Rolling Stone listed the record as #99 on a list of the 100 best albums of the &amp;rsquo ; 90s , saying that Wareham &amp;ldquo ; made his name with the Eighties dream-pop trio Galaxie 500 , but he really found his muse in these scandalously beautiful guitar ballads . &amp;rdquo ; Seattle-raised singer/songwriter Madeline Kenney opens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;36655;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;36690;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36726;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36762;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Feb Fest&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 5-8 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Pacific Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Info : &lt;/strong&gt; $15 at &lt;a href= " http : //pacifictheatre.org " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;36792;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stone&amp;rsquo ; s Throw Productions presents three plays curated by Pacific Theatre&amp;rsquo ; s 2019/20 apprentices . In Marsha Norman&amp;rsquo ; s 1980 bittersweet comedy Third and Oak : The Laundromat , a retired teacher meets a reckless young woman in a Kentucky laundromat in the middle of the night ( Ariel Slack is pictured ) . In Dorothy Parker&amp;rsquo ; s Here We Are ( originally a short story published in 1931 ) , an awkward young couple head to New York for their honeymoon . And , in Nosegate &amp;mdash ; an original play written and performed by acting apprentice Chantal Gallant &amp;mdash ; Santa&amp;rsquo ; s favourite reindeer Rudolph files a harassment complaint . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36826;TOOLONG ... title= " Picture credit : Alexandre Gilbert . " alt= " Picture credit : Alexandre Gilbert . " data-portal-copyright= " Alexandre Gilbert " **28;36862;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1202068 " /&gt; **26;36892;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 12-15 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Cultch &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; from $26 at &lt;a href= " https : **31;36920;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;36953;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Six of Montr&amp;eacute ; al&amp;rsquo ; s best street dancers form human structures that shift , alter , and evolve in synergy with the music in this production , choreographed by Emmanuelle L&amp;ecirc ; Phan and Elon H&amp;ouml ; glund of Montr&amp;eacute ; al&amp;rsquo ; s Tentacle Tribe . Elements of contemporary dance , street dance and martial arts are combined in a physical score that echoes the pulse of respiration as inhalations and exhalations sculpt the dancers&amp;rsquo ; gestures and movements . &amp;ldquo ; The title Ghost alludes to a formless state of being , but can also refer to blockages or traumas , as one faces their ghosts , &amp;rdquo ; the choreographers say in a statement . &amp;ldquo ; Dancers , and also other artists , often talk about being &amp;lsquo ; in the zone , &amp;rsquo ; connecting or catching the ghost . This state transcends verbal communication but is felt and used as a channel for creativity . &amp;rdquo ; Tentacle Tribe&amp;rsquo ; s other shows include the award-winning Threesixnine. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe src= " https : **34;36986;TOOLONG ... width= " 640 " height= " 360 " frameborder= " 0 " allow= " autoplay ; fullscreen " allowfullscreen title= " Ghost- by Tentacle Tribe " &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37022;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37058;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Rock ' n ' Roll Circus&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 8 at 8 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Railway Stage and Beer Caf&amp;eacute ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $10 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;37088;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;37123;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver-based Rock N&amp;rsquo ; Roll Circus celebrates the release of its second album Bottle Rockets and Cherry Bombs . The band&amp;rsquo ; s sound recalls &amp;lsquo ; 70s FM radio staples like Bad Company ; on the new record&amp;rsquo ; s bandcamp page , the description likens the release to Stephen Stills&amp;rsquo ; Manassas and the Black Crowes&amp;rsquo ; Amorica , thanks to &amp;ldquo ; more emphasis on extended guitar solos and percussion . &amp;rdquo ; West Coast music scene vets Chris Woudstra , Jeremy Holmes , John Oulton , Liam MacDonald , Aaron Moser , and Scott Smith make up the group . Local hard rockers La Chinga and singer/songwriter Jody Peck are also on the bill . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;37151;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;37186;TOOLONG &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37222;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37264;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Stephanie IpMon , 03 Feb 2020 15:39:35 **45;37761;TOOLONG ... **25;37808;TOOLONG composer 's work the latest to be celebrated by Canadian Music Centrehttp : **34;37835;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;37871;TOOLONG ... title= " Composer Ross Alden . Pic credit : Elizabeth Carmack. " alt= " Composer Ross Alden . Pic credit : Elizabeth Carmack. " data-portal-copyright= " Elizabeth Carmack " **28;37907;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1200206 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Ross Alden : 100th Birthday Celebration&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 10 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Murray Adaskin Salon , 837 Davie St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; From $10 , &lt;a href= " http : **28;37937;TOOLONG " &gt;m ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Four seasons ago the British Columbia branch of the Canadian Music Centre inaugurated a series of regular season concerts devoted to the works of composers in our region . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The idea was to start by celebrating milestone years for CMC member composers . Coming up is a 100th anniversary program devoted to the music of Ross Alden. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After coming up with the idea , centre regional director Sean Bickerton adopted , pretty much by necessity , a strategy for an intimate series . The Murray Adaskin Salon is just under 50 seats , a decidedly urban sort of space but just right for many events that would be swamped by larger halls . Over the seasons the CMC initiative has become a stellar example of how to do important work on a small scale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This season there have even been a few breakout concerts : a dual celebration of Jordan Nobles and Jocelyn Morlock&amp;rsquo ; s 50th birthdays at The Annex in December ; and there&amp;rsquo ; s an event celebrating Ernst Schneider&amp;rsquo ; s 80th in Penticton on Feb. 8 , including the premiere of Schneider&amp;rsquo ; s Second Piano Concerto . On March 30 , UBC hosts a 70th birthday concert for Stephen Chatman. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But back to the very rare recital devoted to the music of Alden ( 1920&amp;ndash ; 2008 ) . He had many professional roles and two names : as Murray Carmack he was an educator ( two texts he worked on became standard curriculum for B.C. students in the 1950s and &amp;rsquo ; 60s ) , critic ( for The London Times ) , scholar , and affiliate pianist/composer at Rudolf Steiner&amp;rsquo ; s Anthroposophical Society in Switzerland . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He returned to Canada and taught at New Westminster&amp;rsquo ; s Douglas College . As a composer he used the name Ross Alden , writing some of his most impressive music in retirement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The CMC took the unorthodox step of making Alden a posthumous affiliate composer and it is fitting they should celebrate him with performances of his chamber music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37967;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;38009;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;38045;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;38073;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsMusicVancouver My Chemical Romance fans devastated after Americans-only Ticketmaster glitchhttp : **34;38357;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38393;TOOLONG ... title= " Lead singer Gerard Way from My Chemical Romance in concert in Montreal on May 09 , 2007. " alt= " Lead singer Gerard Way from My Chemical Romance in concert in Montreal on May 09 , 2007. " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;38429;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204867 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hundreds of My Chemical Romance fans in Vancouver &lt;a href= " https : **32;38459;TOOLONG ... that they 're not OK&lt;/a&gt; after a glitch shut them out of Friday&amp;rsquo ; s mad scramble for tickets to the band&amp;rsquo ; s closest reunion tour date . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After seven years on hiatus , the American emo rock band announced a full reunion tour last week , with September and October dates in 21 North American cities . Canadian fans out east were delighted to learn that My Chemical Romance would play &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; Toronto 's Scotiabank Arena on Sept. 14 ; Canadian fans on the West Coast , however , were disappointed to learn that the band 's closest stop was &lt;/span&gt; Tacoma on Oct. 4. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But on Friday things got worse for Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s MCR-my when Ticketmaster Canada inadvertently limited ticket sales for the Tacoma show , which went on sale at noon , to customers with an American billing address . The show was sold out within the hour , leaving hundreds of local emo fans , well , quite emotional . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m pretty devastated right now , &amp;rdquo ; confessed Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Michelle Hanley , 26 , a longtime fan who said she still listens to a My Chemical Romance album every day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38493;TOOLONG ... title= " My Chemical Romance in concert at Rogers Arena in Vancouver , B.C. , on August 31 , 2011. " alt= " My Chemical Romance in concert at Rogers Arena in Vancouver , B.C. , on August 31 , 2011. " data-portal-copyright= " Steve Bosch " **28;38529;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204868 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hanley and her friends were more than ready for Friday&amp;rsquo ; s ticket release , but they weren&amp;rsquo ; t prepared for the Ticketmaster glitch. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I ( was ) trying to buy tickets to nine different shows in nine different cities , with the focus on the Tacoma show , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; So I had three friends on three different phones and a laptop all trying to get tickets , and then we got through right away for floor tickets in the pit , and when the tickets were in the checkout and confirmed , there was no option for a Canadian address &amp;mdash ; you could only have an American billing address . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of the band&amp;rsquo ; s other tour dates &amp;mdash ; or at least the other eight that Hanley was watching &amp;mdash ; included an option for Canadian billing , an observation corroborated by other local fans watching multiple markets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was only the Tacoma show , &amp;rdquo ; said Kelli Black . &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Like a personal attack on Vancouver . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Black said the glitch was &amp;ldquo ; terrifying&amp;rdquo ; , but the diehard fan was able to confirm her seats with only seconds to go before she lost her place in the queue . Hanley , meanwhile , could only watch helplessly as her dream seats to see the band she loves &amp;ldquo ; in an incredibly embarrassing way&amp;rdquo ; went to someone else . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; By the time we phoned a friend with an American address , the ten minutes of time that we had to purchase tickets was up , and we lost them , &amp;rdquo ; Hanley said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the end , Hanley did manage to score tickets to the show using a fake zip code , but it was close . The tickets , unfortunately , were not &amp;mdash ; they&amp;rsquo ; re in the Tacoma Dome&amp;rsquo ; s last row . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m glad I got the tickets in the nosebleed ( section ) , &amp;rdquo ; she said , &amp;ldquo ; but I&amp;rsquo ; m the kind of gal that wants to see them in the pits . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For many Vancouver music-lovers , Friday&amp;rsquo ; s snafu &lt;em&gt;was&lt;/em&gt; the pits , and they let the band know . My Chemical Romance acknowledged the issue in a tweet Friday afternoon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt;We have been made aware of a glitch in the system for Tacoma that was making it difficult for people to buy tickets from Canada . This has now been fixed . &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/6LZfEdzyQh " &gt;https : //t.c ... -- My Chemical Romance ( @MCRofficial ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;38559;TOOLONG ... 31 , **33;38594;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;38629;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have been made aware of a glitch in the system for Tacoma that was making it difficult for people to buy tickets from Canada , &amp;rdquo ; they said . &amp;ldquo ; This has now been fixed . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Unfortunately , by then it was too late for many Vancouverites unless they were willing to drastically overspend on the resale market , where prices began skyrocketing just moments after shows all over North America sold out Friday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I saw one that was $10,000 , &amp;rdquo ; said an annoyed Hanley . &amp;ldquo ; They weren&amp;rsquo ; t even the floor . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Richard Earnshaw echoed Hanley&amp;rsquo ; s experience , calling the Ticketmaster glitch that prevented him and his daughter from getting tickets &amp;ldquo ; a catastrophic blunder . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The screen that came up for payment only offered USA customers the opportunity to pay , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Even though I was logged on to my account , with payment options up to date , including credit card , it would not take payment . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I and hundreds of others lost their tickets . " data-portal-copyright= " VISUAL THOUGHT " **28;39005;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1195327 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Propeller hosted by David Ward and Golden Future&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;39035;TOOLONG : Feb. 5 , 12 , 19 , 23 , 9:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;39070;TOOLONG : Guilt and Co. , 1 Alexander St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : By donation ( $15 minimum suggested ) at &lt;a href= " https : **33;39106;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;39141;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; R&amp;amp ; B , funk and soul fans know to roll underground downtown at Guilt and Co . The cool club below street level in Gastown hosts regular live music that favours grooves over guitar solos , and has developed a solid scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Every year , that flies off the hook in February when singer-songwriter David Ward + Golden Future host the annual Propeller concert series . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since its debut , this event has hosted a who&amp;rsquo ; s who of local musicians as well as reaching out across North America to showcase some serious heavyweights you wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t expect to see in the intimate confines of a small Vancouver club . With Ward as MC and his backing band Golden Fortune keeping the music flowing , Propeller 3 really ups the ante . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First up is L.A. keyboard ace Brandon Coleman ( Feb. 5 ) . The keyboardist best known for his work with Kamasi Washington and Childish Gambino dropped his raved-about album Resistance in 2018 on the trendsetting Afro-futurist label Brainfeeder. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;39170;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;39206;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Following Coleman is the latest Brainfeeder label signee Salami Rose Joe Louis ( Feb. 12 ) . Massachusetts hip-hopper Makadi holds court on Feb. 19 and B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s Alexis Young ( Blonde Diamond ) closes out the series on Feb. 23. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Postmedia talked with Ward from his present base of operations in Bristol , U.K. , about Propeller , and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39242;TOOLONG ... title= " David Ward . " alt= " David Ward . " data-portal-copyright= " Alex Townsend " **28;39278;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1195328 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;strong&gt;How hard is it to book a series on the West Coast from a location on the River Avon in Southwest England ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;39308;TOOLONG : Vancouver is something of a niche market for what gets called neo-soul or future R&amp;amp ; B , but there is a lot more acceptance of it here in the U.K. At the same time that we launched Propeller to showcase the best and brightest in the talented local and North American scene , I decided to move to Bristol to further my career . Now I&amp;rsquo ; m doing this crazy thing of jumping back and forth which is , er , challenging at times . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What made you decide to launch Propeller ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;39340;TOOLONG : It was a twofold idea about expanding the opportunities for creative community in Vancouver over here and also &amp;mdash ; without wanting to sound too self-important &amp;mdash ; helping to build the cultural cachet of the city with a great series that anyone can come to see . We have a suggested donation price , but it&amp;rsquo ; s pretty much pay what you can , to ensure access . ( Note : Partial proceeds go to the SPCA. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you envision some kind of expansion from just one series into multiple events ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;39372;TOOLONG : The format from here on out , pending schedules , is going to be four artists a season and we are trying to do it quarterly . So the next would be April/May . Ultimately , I would love to expand this and have an annual showcase at the Commodore . It has to grow another few feet before that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Getting A-list buzzworthy artists such as Brandon Coleman certainly does n't hurt that name recognition . How did you manage to bring him up for this ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;39404;TOOLONG : I had a connection with Patrice Quinn , who is also in Kamasi&amp;rsquo ; s band and sang on my album &lt;a href= " https : **33;39436;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Violet , Gold + Rose&lt;/a&gt; . She played Propeller 1 and was amazing , so I asked her about Brandon and we connected and here he is , as well as Salami Rose Joe Louis , who has this great Billie Holiday voice paired with this jazzy space funk. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;39471;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;39507;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You and your band Golden Future are backing all of these artists when they play . Is it safe to say that this ups your game a lot ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;39543;TOOLONG : For sure , that was another idea behind doing the series . We put in a ton of work and , obviously , it&amp;rsquo ; s not really paying off in any financial way yet . But to be able to meet with these amazing artists and make music with them is amazing . We&amp;rsquo ; re also in the midst of recording the next album , and a lot of what I&amp;rsquo ; ve written has been for Propeller . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;39575;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;39617;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;39968;TOOLONG " **46;39998;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynFri , 31 Jan 2020 19:28:37 **45;40046;TOOLONG ... things to know about Concrete and Glass by Nicolas Godinhttp : **35;40093;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;40130;TOOLONG ... title= " Nicolas Godin , member of the French band Air , has released his solo album Concrete and Glass . " alt= " Nicolas Godin , member of the French band Air , has released his solo album Concrete and Glass . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40166;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1195477 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Concrete and Glass&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Nicolas Godin &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Because Music&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nicolas Godin dropped his first solo album in 2015 . Counterpoint explored intersections between the classical genius of Bach and the cosmopolitan synth-cool that fans the world over would expect from the founder of the Versailles duo Air . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For his latest recording , Godin crafted 10 tracks inspired by &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;architectural reference points : mounted with minimalist precision and delivered with an abundance of pop warmth . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Needless to say , the vast majority of listeners wo n't be likely to grasp which blueprints or existing structures inspired which song , but it 's safe to say that you could play this lounge-ready record in any of them and it would pair perfectly . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are five things to know about Concrete and Glass : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 . Guest singers&lt;/strong&gt; . The title track features the vintage Vocoder-treated vocals that were all over Air albums such as Moon Safari and Love 2 . But over half of the songs , such as the romantic We Forget to Love ( feat . Kadjha Bonet ) or percolating The Foundation ( feat . Cola Boyy ) , are clean , clear Euro-soul free from any modern window dressing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 . Back to Your Heart&lt;/strong&gt; . Featuring Kate NV , this loping mid-tempo pop tune is like Godin decided to channel 10cc for 4:47 . It&amp;rsquo ; s more repetitive than it needs to be , but the electric piano groove is pretty sweet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 . Time On My Hands ( feat . Kirin J Callinan ) &lt;/strong&gt; . Air always had a rear-view take on pop , favouring a kind of late Seventies studio balladry crossed with plenty of progressive rock tricks , too . Case in point , the David Gilmore style guitar in this echoey tune . It&amp;rsquo ; s almost annoyingly slow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 . The Foundation ( feat . Cola Boyy ) &lt;/strong&gt; . Could a more appropriately titled track turn up on an album inspired by architecture ? Godin is on record stating that &amp;ldquo ; &amp;ldquo ; Pierre Koenig&amp;rsquo ; s Case Study House #21 was the starting point for this song . &amp;rdquo ; While nothing in the easygoing song invokes this L.A.-based house in the Hollywood Hills , it&amp;rsquo ; s a cool tune . The house listed in 2016 for under $4 million . Godin missed out on not coming to look at more expensive iconic Vancouver teardowns for inspiration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 . Le Corbusier **26;40196;TOOLONG . Godin is an architecture grad who was deeply inspired by Charles-&amp;Eacute ; douard Jeanneret , the Swiss-French architect also known as Le Corbusier . So he comes by his concept from deep understanding rather than being a dilettante . In 2019 , the musician was asked to compose a tribute to Le Corbusier to be part of a soundtrack to site-specific installations across the globe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;40224;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;40260;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also reviewed this week : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ben Williams&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;I Am A Man &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;Rainbow Blonde Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The title of the latest from DC-based Grammy-winning bassist/composer Williams references the 1968 African-American sanitation workers&amp;rsquo ; strike in Memphis , TN . Over the course of the 10 tracks , he delves into bongo-driven deep grooves while declaring the need for love ( If You Hear Me ) , for freedom ( March On ) and standing up for future generations ( Take It From Me/The Death of Emmett Till ) . With its dense layers , silk soul and jazz-funk backing , this falls right in with classics such as Marvin Gaye&amp;rsquo ; s What&amp;rsquo ; s Going On or the work of Curtis Mayfield . Highly recommended . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;40296;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;40332;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Dan Deacon&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Mystic Familiar&lt;/strong&gt; **27;40368;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Opening with lunging piano that keeps multiplying into circling crescendos , Become a Mountain is spectacularly cinematic . That sets a tone for the latest from this experimental electropop progenitor . His sound ranges from almost early Eno-style pop ( Sat By a Tree ) , to fully realized multi-movement synthesized soundscapes ( Arp I &amp;ndash ; III ) coupled with often robotic vocals . It&amp;rsquo ; s far more mature and pristine than his shambolic early work , but adventurous arrangements and sonic architecture still sit most comfortably in the world of the outsider electronic music scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; March 21 , Venue , 881 Granville . Tickets and info : $24.83 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;40397;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;40432;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;40460;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;40496;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Jason McMahon&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Odd West&lt;/strong&gt; **29;40532;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Guitarist McMahon has plied his trade on electric guitar in bands ranging from Skeletons to Janka Nabay and the Bubu Gang , but this is an open , acoustic album that ranges from near ambient folk ( The Ballad of Fat Lightning ) to Brazilian-tinged handclaps-and-choruses in If It Rhymes , It&amp;rsquo ; s True . The music was developed over a time performing at family gatherings , house concerts and similarly small shows as he honed various finger picking techniques and explored non-standard tunings . The result is 14 mostly instrumental pieces that go down very easily . Art music you could play for your parents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;40563;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;40599;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Ross The Boss&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Born Of Fire ! &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt;AFM Records&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As a founding member of both The Dictators and Manowar , Ross &amp;ldquo ; The Boss&amp;rdquo ; Friedman played a key role in the development of what would become thrash , combining fast punk styles and attitude with the precision hard rock of the Seventies . His solo releases with his band haven&amp;rsquo ; t had the same impact as his Seventies and Eighties work , but on its fourth release , the group shreds . I Am the Sword is a classic , and there is a lot on this 12-tune release to make a headbanger smile . Judas Priest fans , in particular , will really want to give this a spin . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Feb. 11 , Rickshaw Theatre , 254 E. Hastings .
